We further develop a practical extension to two-dimensional problems using line-by-line reconstruction in each coordinate direction. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed method reduces errors by up to one or two orders of magnitude for inviscid transport problems and significantly outperforms total variation regularization in both numerical accuracy and the sharp resolution of discontinuities.

We treat the signature map as a continuous matrix indexed by parameters and space, construct a low-rank skeleton decomposition using a maximal-volume criterion, and learn the parameter-to-coefficient map with a neural network for efficient non-intrusive online evaluation. Abstract: We develop a variational nonlocal phase-field model for dynamic fracture in elastic solids. The proposed formulation is distinguished by three main features. First, the model is formulated through nonlocal kinematics and kernel-dependent function spaces, allowing weaker regularity requirements while recovering the classical local theory as the nonlocal interaction domain vanishes. Second, a nonlocal crack-surface functional is introduced as an integral counterpart of the Ambrosio--Tortorelli regularization, so that the characteristic length of the diffusive crack is implicitly determined by the nonlocal interaction domain rather than by a prescribed length scale. Third, the degraded nonlocal elastic energy and the nonlocal crack-surface functional are combined into a variationally consistent dynamic fracture system, consisting of a nonlocal momentum balance and an irreversible nonlocal gradient-flow evolution law for the phase field. Quantitatively, the predicted crack-tip velocities remain below $0.6c_R$ in the dynamic branching and shear-loading tests, and the shear-loading benchmark gives an inclined crack path of approximately $48^\circ$, consistent with the characteristic Kalthoff--Winkler fracture pattern.

Conclusion: NLF提供了一个统一、高效且经验线性时间的凸网络流均衡求解框架，显著优于现有最先进方法，并具备多商品流和最大流等扩展能力。

Abstract: We present NLF (Nonlinear Laplacian Flow), a unified framework and linear-time solver for convex network-flow equilibria. Congestion routing, minimum-delay routing, and maximum flow share one form: the nonlinear graph Laplacian $Bρ(B^Tφ)=αd$, where a monotone edge law $ρ_e$ encodes the physics (undirected graphs; directed variants are future work). NLF solves it by a damped chord-Newton iteration whose frozen linearization -- a weighted graph Laplacian -- is inverted by a near-linear Laplacian solver (default: approximate Cholesky, LAMG+ interchangeable). The nonlinear solve costs $2$--$4$ linear Laplacian solves, making the wall-clock empirically $O(m)$ in the edge count $m$ (not a proved bound). On single-commodity congestion (BPR cost), NLF converges on all 2,003 SuiteSparse corpus graphs up to $1.8\times10^7$ edges. Against a state-of-the-art interior-point method, NLF is a median $2.6\times$ faster where both converge and $>45\times$ on poorly-separable graphs where the IPM's direct core is superlinear; against L-BFGS, a median $4.2\times$ faster and the only solver to finish on the 90 hardest instances. A multicommodity extension routes $K$ commodities through one shared hierarchy at $O(Km)$ per step. The same machinery recovers the exact max-flow as a short sequence of Laplacian solves, with the cut potential as a by-product. Code: https://github.com/orenlivne/nlf

</details>


### [95] [Weighted Derivative Histopolation on Arbitrary Grids: Admissibility and Exact Factorizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02105)
*Allal Guessab,Federico Nudo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区间族的加权导数直方图插值框架，通过对导数加权的积分矩和标量归一化来确定多项式，给出了该问题适定性的充要条件，并在Chebyshev配置下导出了对角Gram矩阵的显式结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多项式插值方法依赖于函数值采样，而本文希望利用导数在区间上的加权积分矩（即直方图信息）来重构多项式，这在实际应用中（如涉及积分测量数据的场景）具有更广泛的适用性。

Method: 建立加权导数直方图插值框架：自由度包括一个标量归一化项和导数在预设区间族上的加权积分矩。通过分析区间族对多项式空间的分离性，证明当区间族能通过加权矩分离至多N-1次多项式且标量归一化在常数上非零时，该格式在Π_N上是唯一可解的。进一步将区间族的可容许性归结为与网格相关的区间矩阵的非奇异性，并在Jacobi权函数下展示矩阵的块结构，在Chebyshev情形下导出对角Gram矩阵。

Result: 给出了插值问题适定性的充要条件，即区间族通过加权矩分离多项式（确定至多相差一个常数）加上非零的标量归一化。可容许区间族可由固定网格构造，其充要条件是区间矩阵非奇异。在四类经典Chebyshev族下，通过选择合适的多项式基可得到对角化的Gram矩阵，该对角性依赖于权函数、基和网格的协同选择。数值实验展示了在不同Jacobi参数和节点分布下的方法行为。

Conclusion: 本文建立的加权导数直方图插值框架提供了利用导数区间积分矩进行多项式重构的完整理论，其充要条件清晰，且通过Chebyshev配置实现了显式对角化结构，为后续实际应用（如数值积分、谱方法等）奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a weighted derivative histopolation framework on families of intervals. The degrees of freedom consist of one scalar normalization and weighted integral moments of the derivative over a prescribed family of subintervals. We prove that the resulting scheme is unisolvent on $Π_N$ when the interval family separates polynomials of degree at most $N-1$ through weighted moments and the normalization is nonzero on constants. Thus, the derivative moments determine the polynomial up to an additive constant, and the scalar normalization fixes this remaining degree of freedom. This gives a sharp criterion for the well-posedness of the interpolation problem and a complete characterization of the admissible scalar normalizations. We then show how admissible families of intervals can be constructed from a fixed grid. When the endpoints of the intervals belong to the grid, admissibility is reduced to the nonsingularity of an interval matrix associated with the family, which depends only on the representation of the intervals in terms of consecutive cells. For Jacobi weights, the associated data matrices have a natural block structure in Jacobi polynomial bases, and the reduced derivative matrix can be expressed in terms of shifted Jacobi moment matrices. We next study Chebyshev configurations in which this structure becomes explicit. For the four classical Chebyshev families, suitable polynomial bases lead to diagonal Gram matrices for the reduced derivative matrices. We show that this diagonal structure depends on the simultaneous choice of the weight, the basis, and the grid. Numerical experiments on equispaced and Chebyshev--Lobatto nodes show the behaviour of the method for different interval families and for different Jacobi parameters.

</details>


### [96] [Unconditional Optimal Error Estimates and Energy Stability for a Linearly Implicit Mass-Lumped Projection Finite Element Method for the Harmonic Map Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02179)
*Yongyong Cai,Xingwei Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于单位球面调和热流的线性隐式质量集中有限元方法，该方法通过线性预测器与节点投影相结合，精确保持节点处单位长度约束，在笛卡尔矩形网格上实现了无条件离散能量耗散律，并证明了无需时间步长与网格尺寸耦合条件的 O(Δt+h²) 和 O(Δt+h) 最优误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对热流方程映射到单位球面的问题，现有数值方法在处理单位长度约束时存在精度或稳定性限制，作者旨在设计一种既能精确保持节点约束、又具备无条件稳定性和最优收敛阶的高效数值格式。

Method: 方法由两步组成：(1) 基于叉积重构方程的线性隐式质量集中预测器，该预测器等价于带修正项（强制节点正交性）的原始格式质量集中离散化，以及切平面方案；(2) 节点投影至单位球面。关键创新在于质量和刚度项中一致使用离散内积，从而导出节点正交关系，使辅助解位于单位球面之上或之外，保证投影良定性。在笛卡尔矩形/长方体张量积网格上，投影在离散Dirichlet能量下非扩张，实现无条件离散能量耗散。误差分析结合投影误差收缩性、求积一致性估计、基于边的消去恒等式以及控制非线性项的自举论证。

Result: 对于足够光滑的解，方法在无需时间步长与网格尺寸耦合条件下达到：ℓ∞(0,T;L²)范数下 O(Δt+h²) 收敛阶，ℓ²(0,T;H¹)范数下 O(Δt+h) 收敛阶。数值实验验证了预测的收敛率和离散能量衰减。

Conclusion: 该线性隐式质量集中有限元方法成功解决了单位球面调和热流的数值求解问题，兼具精确约束保持、无条件稳定性（能量耗散）和最优收敛阶，为几何流问题提供了一种高效可靠的数值框架。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a linearly implicit mass-lumped finite element method for the heat flow of harmonic maps into the unit sphere. The method consists of a linear predictor followed by a nodal projection and therefore preserves the unit-length constraint exactly at all finite element nodes. The predictor is derived from a cross-product reformulation of the equation and is shown to be equivalent to a mass-lumped discretization of the original formulation with a correction term enforcing nodal orthogonality, as well as to a tangent plane scheme. A key ingredient is the consistent use of the discrete inner product in both the mass and stiffness terms. This yields a nodal orthogonality relation implying that the auxiliary solution lies on or outside the unit sphere at every node. Consequently, the projection is well defined and the projected error satisfies a contraction property in the discrete \(L^2\)-norm. On Cartesian rectangular and cuboidal tensor-product meshes, the nodal projection is also nonexpansive in a discrete Dirichlet energy, which gives an unconditional discrete energy dissipation law. For sufficiently smooth solutions, we prove optimal error estimates without any coupling condition between the time step and the mesh size: the method converges with order \(O(Δt+h^2)\) in \(\ell^\infty(0,T;L^2)\) and order \(O(Δt+h)\) in \(\ell^2(0,T;H^1)\). The proof combines the projected-error contraction, quadrature consistency estimates, edge-based cancellation identities, and a bootstrap argument for controlling nonlinear terms. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence rates and the discrete energy decay.

</details>


### [97] [A highly efficient iterative approach for inverse acoustic obstacle scattering problems in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02180)
*Zhiyong Cheng,Heping Dong,Lu Zhao*

TL;DR: 本文针对三维声学逆障碍散射问题，提出了一种基于同伦曲面边界积分的高效迭代重建方法，完全避免了奇异性处理，并证明了Fréchet导数的单射性和稠密值域性质以确保线性化等效数据方程的可解性，同时证明了同伦曲面产生的散射场可任意逼近精确场。数值实验验证了方法的优越性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决三维声学逆散射问题中基于散射场或有相/无相远场数据的障碍物重建，现有方法常面临奇异性处理的挑战，因此需要一种高效且能完全避免奇异性处理的迭代方法。

Method: 基于同伦曲面上定义的边界积分，提出迭代重建方法，完全避免了奇异性处理；证明了Fréchet导数的单射性和稠密值域性质以确保线性化等效数据方程的可解性。

Result: 证明了同伦曲面产生的散射场可任意逼近精确场；数值实验验证了所提方法的优越性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于同伦曲面边界积分的迭代方法能高效求解三维声学逆障碍散射问题，具有理论保证和良好的数值表现。

Abstract: This paper concerns a three-dimensional inverse acoustic obstacle scattering problem from scattered field or phased/phaseless far-field data. Based on the boundary integral defined on a homothetic surface, we propose a highly efficient iterative approach for obstacle reconstruction that completely avoids dealing with any singularity. Here, the injectivity and dense-range property of the Fréchet derivative have been proved to ensure the solvability of the linearized equivalent data equation. We also prove that the scattered field generated by the homothetic surface can arbitrarily approximate the exact one. Numerical experiments are presented to verify the superiority and robustness of the proposed approach.

</details>

*Lu Zhao,Heping Dong,Zhiyong Cheng*

TL;DR: 本文针对时域三维声学障碍散射的正反问题，提出了一种基于延迟势边界元法和卷积求积的迭代重构算法，通过引入同伦曲面上的延迟边界积分，将时域问题转化为s域非奇异积分，并证明了同伦曲面产生的散射场收敛于精确场，同时采用增量截断技术提升反演稳定性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决时域三维声学障碍散射问题中，刚性障碍物形状和位置的重构困难，特别是传统方法在处理时域延迟势积分时计算复杂度高、稳定性差的问题。

Method: 采用卷积求积和Galerkin方法离散延迟势边界积分方程；引入同伦曲面上的延迟边界积分，将时域积分转化为s域积分系统（仅含非奇异积分）；推导关于边界的Fréchet导数；提出增量截断技术提升算法稳定性。

Result: 证明了同伦曲面生成的散射场在时域收敛于精确场；数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于同伦曲面和卷积求积的迭代方法能够高效、稳定地重构时域三维声学障碍的边界形状和位置，且s域积分的非奇异特性大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: This paper concerns the three-dimensional forward and inverse acoustic obstacle scattering problem in the time domain. For the forward problem, a retarded potential formulation discretized by convolution quadrature and Galerkin methods is introduced. By introducing the retarded boundary integral defined on a homothetic surface, we propose a novel time-domain convolution quadrature based iterative method to reconstruct both the shape and location of a rigid obstacle. The retarded integral in the time domain is reformulated into a system of integrals in the s-domain. The resulting s-domain integrals are very fast to compute, as they only involve non-singular integrals over the homothetic surfaces. Moreover, the Fréchet derivative with respect to the boundary can be derived straightforwardly. We also prove that the scattered field generated by the homothetic surface converges to the exact field in the time domain. To improve the stability of the inversion algorithm, an incremental truncation technique is proposed, and numerical experiments confirm the effectiveness and robustness of our method.

</details>


### [99] [A Stable Boundary Element Method for Reliable Long-Time Industrial Sound Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02308)

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于长时间工业声发射问题的稳定时空公式，基于三维声波方程的超奇异边界积分算子Galerkin格式，通过数值实验证明其稳定性、准确性，并与实际物理测量结果高度吻合。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用三维声波方程的适定Galerkin时空公式，结合超奇异边界积分算子，构建稳定的时间步进方案。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在复杂工业几何声学问题中稳定且精确；与物理声学测量结果高度一致；计算效率满足实际工程需求。

Conclusion: 提出的时空公式是工业声学问题长时间模拟的有效方案，优于现有替代方法，具备工程实用性。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate a stable space-time formulation for long-time industrial sound emission problems. To this end, we use a well-posed Galerkin formulation in space and time of the acoustic wave equation in $\mathbb{R}^3$, involving a hypersingular boundary integral operator. Our numerical experiments confirm that the resulting time stepping scheme is stable and accurate for complex acoustic problems in industrial geometries, in contrast to alternative well-known schemes. The proposed method is shown to be efficient for real-world problems, and we obtain very good agreement with physical acoustic measurements.

</details>


### [100] [A Unified CutFEM Formulation for Finite-Strain Elasticity: Energy Minimisation and Corner Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02334)
*Michał Tomasz Wichrowski,Ella Godiva Noomen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全变分、模型无关的CutFEM公式，用于有限应变弹性力学。该方法通过自动微分从标量能量密度生成应力与弹性张量，实现了模型无关性。理论分析证明了切割无关的 coercivity、连续性和条件数界，并通过数值实验验证了最优h-收敛性。在混合Dirichlet-Neumann交界处，方法精度受角点奇异性限制，但局部网格细化可恢复最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CutFEM方法在有限应变弹性问题中缺乏一种完全变分、模型无关的构造方式，且未与自动微分结合。手动推导不同超弹性模型的应力与切线张量繁琐易错，亟需一种能自动生成这些量的通用框架。

Method: 提出一个增广能量泛函（包含体超弹性能量、Nitsche边界弱施加项和ghost-penalty稳定项），其驻点条件定义离散问题。残量和对称切线通过逐次变分得到，自动微分从标量能量密度生成第一Piola-Kirchhoff应力张量和弹性张量。线性化问题分析建立了切割无关的 coercivity、连续性和O(h^{-2})条件数界，通过Brezzi-Rappaz-Raviart框架得到准最优收敛定理。使用Kolosov-Muskhelishvili特征方程量化混合边界交界处的精度极限。

Result: 方法在光滑测试问题上对线性、二次和三次单元达到最优h-收敛。混合Dirichlet-Neumann交界处的角点奇异性使收敛率出现上限，该上限对 fitted 和 unfitted 方法相同。局部网格细化可解除此限制，unfitted 离散继承了恢复的最优收敛率和切割无关常数。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了结合能量驱动、模型无关构造、自动微分和边界精度分析的unfitted有限应变格式，为CutFEM在非线性弹性问题中的稳健应用提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: We present a fully variational, model-independent formulation of the Cut Finite Element Method (CutFEM) for finite-strain elasticity. The discrete problem is the stationarity condition of a augmented energy functional consisting of the bulk hyperelastic energy, the Nitsche terms that impose the boundary conditions weakly, and the ghost-penalty stabilisation. The residual and the (symmetrised) tangent follow from this functional by successive variations. Automatic differentiation (AD) generates the first Piola--Kirchhoff stress tensor and the elasticity tensor directly from the scalar energy density, avoiding manual re-derivation when exchanging hyperelastic models. To our knowledge, this is the first unfitted finite-strain scheme combining an energy-only, model-independent construction with AD and an accuracy analysis at unfitted boundaries.
  Analysis of the linearised problem solved at each Newton step establishes cut-independent coercivity, continuity, and an $O(h^{-2})$ condition number bound, yielding a quasi-optimal convergence theorem for regular solutions through the Brezzi--Rappaz--Raviart framework. Numerically, the method attains optimal $h$-convergence for linear, quadratic, and cubic elements on a smooth test case. Furthermore, we quantify the method's accuracy limit at mixed Dirichlet--Neumann junctions using the Kolosov--Muskhelishvili characteristic equation. The exact solution's corner singularity caps the convergence rate identically for fitted and unfitted methods. We demonstrate that local mesh refinement removes this bound, with the unfitted discretisation inheriting the recovered optimal rates and cut-independent constants.

</details>


### [101] [Coalesced Matrix-Free Finite Elements in Cell-Wise Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02335)
*Michał Wichrowski*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向GPU的连续高阶有限元方法，以单元局部向量作为场数据的持久主表示，证明在预处理子像连续的前提下，整个柔性共轭梯度迭代可完全在未组装表示上精确执行，通过维度分裂的面交换实现直接刚度求和，数值实验表明该方法在GPU上显著超越现有矩阵无关实现的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法中，单元局部数据仅作为矩阵无关算子评估的临时阶段存在，需要频繁的全局组装与分散/收集操作，导致GPU上的通信开销和存储访问效率低下。作者试图证明单元局部表示可以作为持久的主数据表示，从而简化GPU计算流程并提升性能。

Method: 1) 理论证明：若预处理子像连续，则柔性共轭梯度法可在未组装（单元局部）表示上精确执行，基于原-对偶配对恒等式证明所有Krylov标量值与组装求解一致。2) 直接刚度求和(DSS)实现：采用维度分裂的级联一对一面对交换，顺次轴遍历自然累积边和顶点贡献；非结构宏块界面和h自适应悬挂节点通过不相交拓扑核与影子单元包装处理。3) 存储布局：单元局部存储解耦内存布局与网格拓扑，对比不同分块布局在内存合并与单元连续性间的权衡。

Result: 在当代GPU上的数值实验表明，所提出的算子评估和求解器性能显著超越现有最先进的矩阵无关实现，吞吐量大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明高阶连续有限元的GPU高效实现可以通过将单元局部向量作为持久主表示来实现，将通信限制在预处理子内部，并通过维度分裂的面交换高效完成直接刚度求和，为GPU上的高阶有限元计算提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: We present a GPU-oriented formulation of continuous high-order finite elements in which the redundant, cell-wise (element-local) vector is the persistent primary representation of all field data, rather than a transient stage of matrix-free operator evaluation. We prove that, given a preconditioner whose image is continuous, the entire flexible conjugate gradient iteration can be carried out exactly on this unassembled representation: a simple primal-dual pairing identity shows that all Krylov scalars computed from local data coincide with those of the assembled solve, so inter-element communication is confined entirely to the preconditioner. The required direct stiffness summation (DSS) is then realized without indirect gather-scatter, atomics, or coloring, by a dimensionally-split cascade of one-to-one face exchanges that provably accumulates edge and vertex contributions as a byproduct of sequential axis passes; unstructured macro-block interfaces and $h$-adaptive hanging nodes are handled by disjoint topological kernels and a shadow-cell wrapper that leaves the high-throughput sweeps untouched. The cell-wise storage decouples the memory layout from the mesh topology, and we exploit this freedom to benchmark blocked layouts that trade memory coalescing against element contiguity. Numerical experiments on modern GPUs demonstrate that the resulting operator evaluation and solver outperform state-of-the-art matrix-free implementations, signifficantly exceeding throughput of existing implementations.

</details>


### [102] [Block Preconditioning for Shifted Boundary Method Discretisations of the Stokes Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02336)
*Michał Wichrowski,Ajay Ajith*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对移位边界法（SBM）离散的不可压缩Stokes方程，提出了一种基于速度块和压力质量矩阵作为Schur补近似的块预条件子。通过场值分析证明非对称SBM项是标准鞍点算子的渐近小扰动，在足够细网格上实现网格无关的GMRES收敛。数值实验验证了迭代次数随网格细化保持稳定，同时揭示了粗网格下因分辨率不足导致迭代次数升高的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBM方法虽已广泛应用于不可压缩Stokes方程的求解，但面向该方法的可扩展迭代求解器尚不成熟，现有的算子预条件理论无法直接应用于非对称系统。

Method: 提出块预条件子，以速度块和压力质量矩阵近似Schur补；采用场值分析（field-of-values analysis）而非经典算子预条件，论证非对称SBM项为鞍点算子的小扰动。

Result: 在足够细网格上实现网格无关的GMRES收敛；粗网格下因几何分辨率不足导致迭代次数升高，该伪影随网格细化消失。

Conclusion: 所提出的块预条件子能有效处理SBM离散的Stokes方程非对称性，在充分解析几何的网格上可获得网格无关的迭代性能。

Abstract: The Shifted Boundary Method (SBM) sidesteps body-fitted meshing by shifting boundary conditions onto a surrogate boundary and correcting for the displacement through Taylor expansions. Despite its broad analysis and application, scalable iterative solvers for the incompressible Stokes equations remain underdeveloped. We present a block preconditioner for SBM--Stokes discretisations that uses the velocity block together with a pressure mass matrix as a Schur complement approximation. Because the SBM system is non-symmetric, classical operator preconditioning does not apply directly; a field-of-values analysis instead shows that the non-symmetric SBM contributions act as asymptotically small perturbations of a standard saddle-point operator, yielding mesh-independent GMRES convergence on sufficiently fine meshes. Numerical experiments demonstrate iteration counts under refinement across geometries of increasing complexity. We expose a coarse-mesh regime in which an under-resolved grid produces elevated iteration counts, an artefact of insufficient resolution that vanishes once the mesh captures the geometry.

</details>


### [103] [A PDE-Based Framework for Generative Modeling Beyond Classical Score-Based Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02349)
*Horacio Tettamanti,Michael Herty*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非线性修正的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的生成框架，该框架在正向动力学中展现出凝聚现象（有限时间内损失L2正则性），并由此推导出稳定的逆向偏微分方程以重建初始分布，从而将生成范式扩展到经典基于分数的框架之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的基于分数的生成模型依赖于线性随机微分方程（如扩散过程），本文旨在探索一种非线性动力学机制，通过引入凝聚现象来丰富生成模型的动力学行为，同时保持逆向重建的可解性。

Method: 1) 提出非线性修正的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck动力学，其微观描述为相互作用粒子系统，宏观描述为具有超线性漂移项的非线性Fokker-Planck方程；2) 证明在一定参数和初始质量条件下，正向动力学在有限时间内发生凝聚（L2正则性丧失）；3) 推导稳定的逆向时间偏微分方程，从正向渐近状态重建初始分布；4) 设计正向和逆向过程的数值离散化方案。

Result: 在1D和2D数值实验中，所提方法准确捕捉了连续模型的渐近行为，并成功重建了初始分布。通过生成过程的多次迭代实现了密度过滤的应用。

Conclusion: 该框架为生成模型提供了一种超越分数匹配的新范式，利用非线性动力学中的凝聚现象拓展了可逆生成过程的构建思路，并在数值上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We introduce an alternative generative framework based on a nonlinear modification of the classical Ornstein--Uhlenbeck dynamics. The proposed dynamics admits both a microscopic description through an interacting particle system and, in the mean-field limit, a macroscopic formulation given by a nonlinear Fokker--Planck equation with a superlinear drift term. We show that, for suitable choices of the model parameters and sufficiently large initial mass, the forward dynamics exhibits condensation phenomena by proving the loss of $L^2$ regularity of the solution in finite time. Building upon this formulation, we derive a stabilized reverse-time partial differential equation that reconstructs the initial distribution from the asymptotic state of the forward dynamics, thereby extending the generative paradigm beyond the classical score-based framework. Furthermore, we introduce numerical discretizations of both the forward and reverse processes that accurately capture the asymptotic behavior of the continuous model while successfully reconstructing the initial distribution. Numerical experiments in one and two spatial dimensions validate the proposed methodology and illustrate its application to density filtering through successive iterations of the generative process.

</details>


### [104] [Sobolev stability of the $L^2$-projection on hybrid meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02362)
*Lars Diening,Viktoria Lingert,Tabea Tscherpel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应网格细化中由三角形和凸四边形组成的混合网格上，映射拉格朗日有限元上L²投影的L^p和W^{1,p}稳定性，特别证明了所有K≥2时Q-RG和Q-RB细化的W^{1,2}稳定性，将Ali, Funken和Schmidt（2022）的结果从平行四边形初始网格推广到一般凸四边形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的针对平行四边形初始网格的W^{1,2}稳定性结果（仅适用于2≤K≤9）推广到一般凸四边形混合网格上的所有K≥2情形。

Method: 将Diening, Storn和Tscherpel（2021）的技术扩展到一般凸四边形情形，结合三角形和凸四边形组成的混合网格分析。

Result: 证明了所有K≥2时Q-RG和Q-RB细化的W^{1,2}稳定性，并将适用范围从平行四边形初始网格推广到一般凸四边形混合网格。

Conclusion: 本文所建立的稳定性结果是已有工作的实质性推广，为自适应网格细化中有限元方法的理论分析提供了更一般化的基础。

Abstract: We establish $L^p$- and $W^{1,p}$-stability of the $L^2$-projection onto mapped Lagrange finite elements on hybrid meshes consisting of triangles and convex quadrilaterals arising from adaptive mesh refinement. If $K$ is the (tensor product) degree of polynomials of the discretisation, then we show, in particular, $W^{1,2}$-stability for all $K\geq 2$ for the Q-RG and Q-RB refinements. This extends results by Ali, Funken, and Schmidt (2022) which hold for the range $2 \leq K \leq 9$ for initial meshes consisting of parallelograms. Our proof relies on an extension of the technique by Diening, Storn and Tscherpel (2021) to general convex quadrilaterals.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

### [105] [Utilizing Smoothing Techniques to Bound $|ζ(1+it)|$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01424)
*Andrew Christensen,Kyle Pratt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用平滑技术改进了ζ(1+it)在3≤t≤10⁹范围内的显式上界，证明了对t≥3有|ζ(1+it)| ≤ ½ log t + 1.57，对t≥10⁸有更紧的界|ζ(1+it)| ≤ ⅓ log t + 2 log log t - 1.16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进黎曼ζ函数在临界线附近（实部为1）的显式上界，这对解析数论中素数分布等问题的研究具有重要意义。

Method: 采用平滑技术（smoothing techniques）来优化先前的估计，先前的方法依赖于Riemann-Siegel公式和三角不等式。

Result: 对于t≥3，得到上界|ζ(1+it)| ≤ ½ log t + 1.57；对于t≥10⁸，得到更优的上界|ζ(1+it)| ≤ ⅓ log t + 2 log log t - 1.16。

Conclusion: 通过平滑技术显著改善了ζ(1+it)在指定范围内的显式上界估计。

Abstract: We demonstrate an improved explicit upper bound of $|ζ(1+it)|$ for $3 \leq t \leq 10^9$ using smoothing techniques. Our method sharpens previous bounds relying on the Riemann--Siegel formula and the triangle inequality. In particular, we prove that for $t\geq 3$, \begin{align*}
  |ζ(1+it)| \leq \frac{1}{2}\log t + 1.57 \end{align*} and for $t \geq 10^8$, \[ |ζ(1+it)|\leq \frac{1}{3}\log t + 2\log \log t -1.16 . \]

</details>


### [106] [Necessary and sufficient conditions on the order of a finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$ for the easy identification of primitive polynomials of degree 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01542)
*Gerardo Vega*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了有限域$\mathbb{F}_q$上的二次不可约多项式$x^2+bx+c$（其中$b\neq 0$，$c$为本原元）为本原多项式的充要条件，并由此得到一个新的无限族有限域，其中可简单判定二次不可约多项式是否为本原多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定有限域上不可约二次多项式为本原多项式的充要条件，特别是当常数项为本原元时的情形，并寻找可简单判定此类多项式本原性的无限族有限域。

Method: 通过分析有限域$\mathbb{F}_q$的阶$q$的条件，研究形如$x^2+bx+c$（$b\neq 0$，$c$为本原元）的不可约多项式，推导其为本原多项式的充要条件。

Result: 给出了$q$的充要条件，使得所有满足条件的二次不可约多项式均为本原多项式，并得到一个新的无限族有限域，其中可以另一种简单方式判定二次不可约多项式的本原性。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了特定形式的二次不可约多项式在本原元常数项条件下的本原性，并提供了新的易于判定的无限族有限域。

Abstract: We present the necessary and sufficient conditions on the order $q$ of a finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$ such that every irreducible polynomial of the form $x^2+bx+c \in \mathbb{F}_q[x]$, with $b\neq 0$ and $c$ a primitive element of $\mathbb{F}_q$, is a primitive polynomial. As a by-product of this result, we also present a new infinite family of finite fields $\mathbb{F}_q$ for which it is easy, in a different way, to determine when an irreducible polynomial of degree two is primitive.

</details>


### [107] [Formalized $q$-series: The Rogers-Ramanujan Identities and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01544)
*Kenny Lau,Seewoo Lee,Ken Ono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Lean证明助手中形式化了$q$-级数和基本超几何级数理论，构建了$q$-Pochhammer符号、$q$-二项式系数、Bailey引理等核心原语，并给出了Jacobi三重积公式和Rogers-Ramanujan恒等式的完全验证证明，为模形式、mock theta函数等后续形式化工作建立了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $q$-级数在组合数学、数论和表示理论等领域的交叉中发挥关键作用，但其形式化面临代数恒等式与解析收敛性之间协调的挑战。本文旨在设计可扩展且通用的结构，在Lean证明助手中建立$q$-级数理论的形式化基础。

Method: 采用Lean证明助手进行形式化，重点构造$q$-Pochhammer符号、$q$-二项式系数、Bailey引理等基本原语，通过精心设计可扩展的结构来调和形式代数恒等式与解析收敛性质。

Result: 成功形式化了$q$-级数的核心原语，并提供了Jacobi三重积公式和Rogers-Ramanujan恒等式的完全验证证明。

Conclusion: 本工作为后续mock theta函数、模形式及相关代数结构的形式化建立了严格的计算基础，这些结构在数学和物理学中具有广泛应用。

Abstract: The theory of $q$-series and basic hypergeometric series plays a crucial role at the intersection of combinatorics, number theory, and representation theory. From the classical partition identities of Euler and Jacobi to modern developments in class field theory, vertex operator algebras, and the Monstrous Moonshine conjecture, $q$-series provide the analytic framework for a wide range of profound applications. In this paper, we discuss the formalization of this theory in the Lean proof assistant, a process that requires careful design of scalable and versatile structures to reconcile formal algebraic identities with analytic convergence properties. We address these foundational challenges by focusing on the construction of $q$-Pochhammer symbols, $q$-binomial coefficients, Bailey's Lemma and similar primitives. To demonstrate the utility of this work, we provide fully verified proofs of the Jacobi Triple Product formula and the celebrated Rogers-Ramanujan identities, which serve as both historical and technical benchmarks for the field. This work establishes a rigorous computational foundation for the future formalization of mock theta functions, modular forms, and the diverse algebraic structures that underpin their applications across mathematics and physics.

</details>


### [108] [A Pfaffian Proof and Generalization of a Conjecture of Sun Zhiwei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01695)
*Hong-Ge Chen,Fei Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了奇素数p下，基于Legendre符号χ构造的矩阵D_a(x)和D_a^{(0)}(x)的行列式性质，给出了D_a(0)=0的充要条件，并推导了p≡3 mod 4时显式的Pfaffian平方分解，最终解决了Sun的猜想4.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究由Legendre符号构造的特殊矩阵的行列式性质，特别是其零点条件与平方分解结构，并解决相关的未解决问题（Sun猜想4.1）。

Method: 利用Legendre符号的二次特征、Pfaffian（普法夫型）理论、矩阵分解技巧，以及模p的阶乘性质，对行列式D_a(x)和D_a^{(0)}(x)进行代数推导和分类讨论。

Result: 1) 给出D_a(0)=0的充要条件：p≡3 mod 4且χ(a n!)=1。2) 当p≡3 mod 4时，给出D_a(x)和D_a^{(0)}(x)的显式Pfaffian平方分解。3) 若χ(a n!)=1，则s_p D_a(x)/x = s_p D_a^{(0)}(x)为正整数平方；若χ(a n!)=-1，则存在正整数σ使得s_p D_a(x)=σ^2(nx-1)，s_p D_a^{(0)}(x)=-σ^2(n+(2n+1)x)。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了该类Legendre符号矩阵的行列式结构，并解决了Sun猜想4.1。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime, let $n=(p-1)/2$, and let $χ=(\frac{\cdot}{p})$, with $χ(0)=0$. For $a\in\mathbb F_p^\times$ define \[
  D_a(x)=\det_{1\le i,j\le n}(x+χ(i^2-aj)),
  \qquad
  D_a^{(0)}(x)=\det_{0\le i,j\le n}(x+χ(i^2-aj)). \] We prove \[
  D_a(0)=0
  \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
  p\equiv 3 \pmod 4
  \quad\text{and}\quad
  χ(a n!)=1. \] For $p\equiv3\pmod4$ we also give explicit Pfaffian-square factorizations of $D_a(x)$ and $D_a^{(0)}(x)$. Let $s_p=(-1)^{\lfloor(p+1)/8\rfloor}$. If $χ(a n!)=1$, then $s_pD_a(x)/x=s_pD_a^{(0)}(x)$ is a positive integer square. If $χ(a n!)=-1$, then there is a positive integer $σ$ such that \[
  s_pD_a(x)=σ^2(nx-1),\qquad
  s_pD_a^{(0)}(x)=-σ^2\bigl(n+(2n+1)x\bigr). \] The case $a=n!$ settles Sun's Conjecture 4.1.

</details>


### [109] [On Euclidean systems of ray classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01703)
*Yutaro Matsuno*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将欧几里得系统推广到射线类群上，证明了每个射线类群的欧几里得系统都能生成相应的射线类群，并在广义黎曼假设下证明了对某些全实伽罗瓦数域和奇素数，射线类群的任何生成集都是欧几里得系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Lenstra的欧几里得理想类概念和Treatman的欧几里得系统概念进一步推广到射线类群（ray classes）上，以研究其基本性质。

Method: 首先形式化定义了射线类群的欧几里得系统，然后证明了每个这样的系统都能生成对应的射线类群；接着在GRH假设下，针对全实伽罗瓦数域和特定奇素数，证明了射线类群生成集的欧几里得性质。

Result: (1) 每个射线类群的欧几里得系统都能生成相应的射线类群；(2) 在GRH条件下，若K是n≥3的全实伽罗瓦数域，p是奇有理素数且在K中不完全分裂，则对任意N>0，模(p)^N的射线类群Cl_K^{(p)^N}的任何生成集都是欧几里得系统。

Conclusion: 成功将欧几里得系统理论从理想类群扩展到射线类群，并在GRH下给出了射线类群生成集为欧几里得系统的充分条件。

Abstract: Lenstra introduced the notion of Euclidean ideal classes, and Treatman extended it to Euclidean systems. In this paper, we formulate Euclidean systems for ray classes, and study their basic properties. In particular, we show that every Euclidean system of ray classes generates the corre sponding ray class group. We further prove, assuming GRH, that if $K$ is a totally real Galois number field of degree $n\ge 3$ and $p$ is an odd ratio nal prime which does not split completely in $K$, then for every $N>0$, every generating set of the ray class group $Cl_K^{(p)^N}$ with modulus $(p)^N$ is a Euclidean system.

</details>


### [110] [A Coordinate System for Collatz Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01718)
*Jennifer Williams*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文基于每个奇数可唯一分解为 $n = \lambda \cdot 2^a \cdot 3^b - 1$（$\gcd(\lambda,6)=1$, $a \geq 1$）的 3-光滑分解，将非负整数划分为可数无穷多个无限三角形，每行 $k$ 形成奇偶交替的 Collatz 链。该划分引入坐标系统 $\mathcal{L}_\lambda$，其中 $(a,b)$ 映射到 $(a-1,b+1)$ 的确定性对角流，无需数论输入。在边界 $a=1$ 处，轨迹根据 $\lambda \cdot 3^{b+1} - 1$ 的分解进入另一个骨架。作为应用，证明了主骨架 $\mathcal{L}_1$ 中 $k \equiv 2 \pmod 4$ 且 $k \geq 6$ 的行不含素数，且这是唯一存在完全代数障碍的同余类。所有结果独立于 Collatz 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用 3-光滑分解 ($n = \lambda \cdot 2^a \cdot 3^b - 1$) 建立非负整数的新型划分，将 Collatz 链的奇偶交替性系统化到三角形结构中，为研究整数性质（如素数分布）提供无需依赖数论输入的坐标框架。

Method: 基于每个奇数的唯一 3-光滑分解，定义骨架 $\mathcal{L}_\lambda$ 坐标系统 $(a,b)$，其中行 $k = a+b$ 对应奇偶交替的 Collatz 链；定义确定性对角流映射 $(a,b) \to (a-1,b+1)$；在边界 $a=1$ 处通过因子化 $\lambda \cdot 3^{b+1} - 1$ 实现骨架间跳转。

Result: 证明了主骨架 $\mathcal{L}_1$ 中行 $k \equiv 2 \pmod 4$ 且 $k \geq 6$ 不包含任何素数，且该同余类是唯一具有完全代数障碍的类别。

Conclusion: 提出了一个独立于 Collatz 猜想的新型坐标框架，将 Collatz 链结构与非负整数的几何划分联系起来，并成功应用于素数分布分析，揭示了特定同余类中素数的完全缺失。

Abstract: It is well-established that every odd positive integer $n$ can be written uniquely as $n = λ\cdot 2^a \cdot 3^b - 1$ where $\gcd(λ, 6) = 1$ and $a \geq 1$. Building from this 3-smooth factorization, we introduce a partition of the nonnegative integers into countably many infinite triangles where each row $k$ forms a Collatz chain of alternating parity. The partition admits a coordinate system as a skeleton $\mathcal{L}_λ$ using the pair $(a, b)$ for odd positive integers within a geometric structure where row $k$ corresponds to $k = a + b$. Each position $(a, b)$ maps to $(a-1, b+1)$, a deterministic diagonal flow requiring no number-theoretic input. At the boundary $a = 1$, the trajectory exits to another skeleton depending on the factorization of $λ\cdot 3^{b+1} - 1$. The coordinate system is new. As a concrete application, we prove that rows $k \equiv 2 \pmod 4$ with $k \geq 6$ in the principal skeleton $\mathcal{L}_1$ contain no primes, and show this is the unique residue class admitting complete algebraic obstruction. Our contribution is the framework that makes visible which nonnegative integers these arguments apply to, with all results independent of the Collatz conjecture.

</details>


### [111] [Gross-Zagier formula for the $4, 7$ cases of Sylvester's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01744)
*Hongbo Yin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了CM点与L-函数导数之间的显式Gross-Zagier公式，解决了Sylvester猜想中p≡4,7 mod 9的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前在p≡4,7 mod 9的素数情形下，在椭圆曲线E_{p^i}上构造了CM点，这些点给出了曲线x^3+y^3=p^i上的有理点，从而解决了Sylvester猜想的部分情形。本文旨在建立这些CM点的高度与对应L-函数导数之间的显式关系。

Method: 利用Gross-Zagier公式的理论框架，针对所构造的特定CM点，推导并验证显式的高度公式与L-函数导数的对应关系。

Result: 成功证明了显式的Gross-Zagier公式，建立了CM点的高度与椭圆曲线E_{p^i}的L-函数导数之间的精确联系。

Conclusion: 本文为Sylvester猜想中p≡4,7 mod 9情形的完整证明提供了关键的解析数论基础。

Abstract: In \cite{Yin26}, the author constructed some CM points on the elliptic curves $E_{p^i}:y^2=x^3+\frac{p^{2i}}{4}$ for primes $p\equiv 4,7\mod 9$ and $i=1,2$, which give rational points on the curves $x^3+y^3=p^i$. This solves the $4,7$ cases of Sylvester's conjecture. In this paper, we prove the explicit Gross-Zagier formula relating the height of our CM points and the derivative of the $L$-functions of $E_{p^i}$.

</details>


### [112] [On a Smoothed Walfisz Divisor Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01956)
*Olivier Bordellès,Florian Daval*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究除数函数σ的平滑Dirichlet除数问题，得到了带权重1-x/n的σ(n)求和的全显式渐近公式，并由此推导出Walfisz除数问题中误差项相关积分的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在之前对光滑Dirichlet除数问题研究的基础上，将Dirichlet除数函数τ替换为除数函数σ，旨在消除σ(n)经典平均阶中的困难部分。

Method: 采用平滑权重1-x/n对σ(n)进行加和，利用均值定理和Euler-Maclaurin求和公式推导出全显式的渐近公式。

Result: 得到了σ(n)带平滑权重的求和的全显式渐近公式，并证明了Walfisz除数问题中误差项相关积分的收敛性。

Conclusion: 通过平滑权重方法有效消除了经典平均阶中的困难部分，附录中给出了均值定理和Euler-Maclaurin求和公式的显式结果。

Abstract: This work is in the spirit of our previous investigation on a smooth Dirichlet divisor problem, where we now replace the Dirichlet divisor function $τ$ by the sum-of-divisors function $σ$. We prove a totally explicit asymptotic formula for the sum of $σ(n)$ twisted by the weight $1-x/n$, which enables us to eliminate the difficult part in the classical average order of $σ(n)$. As a corollary, we deduce the convergence of an integral dealing with the error term in the Walfisz divisor problem. We also provide an appendix containing the necessary explicit results derived from the mean value theorem and the Euler-Maclaurin summation formula.

</details>


### [113] [The Eichler--Selberg trace formula for Hilbert cusp forms, the class numbers of quartic CM fields, and their distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01957)
*Seiji Kuga,Andrei Seymour-Howell,Satoshi Wakatsuki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Hurwitz类数到全实域，建立了实二次域上Hilbert尖形式的Eichler-Selberg迹公式，并研究了广义Hurwitz类数的分布、类数关系及Hecke算子迹的数值计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Su在\cite{Su16}中关于全实域上半整数权Cohen型Eisenstein级数构造的启发，旨在将Hurwitz类数推广到全实域，并建立相应的Eichler-Selberg迹公式。

Method: 通过推广Hurwitz类数到全实域（基于四次CM域的类数而非虚二次域的类数），在窄类数为一的实二次域上建立Hilbert尖形式的Eichler-Selberg迹公式，并利用该迹公式研究分布性质、类数关系及进行数值计算。

Result: 建立了实二次域上Hilbert尖形式的Eichler-Selberg迹公式；得到了广义Hurwitz类数的分布性质；证明了类数关系；完成了$\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{5})$和$\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{29})$上Hecke算子迹的数值计算。

Conclusion: 成功将经典Hurwitz类数及Eichler-Selberg迹公式推广到全实域和实二次域情形，为后续研究和数值应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Motivated by Su's construction of Cohen-type Eisenstein series of half-integral weight over totally real number fields \cite{Su16}, we introduce a generalization of Hurwitz class numbers to totally real number fields. Using these generalized Hurwitz class numbers, we establish an Eichler--Selberg trace formula for the space of holomorphic Hilbert cusp forms over real quadratic fields of narrow class number one. While the classical Hurwitz class numbers are defined in terms of class numbers of imaginary quadratic fields, the generalized Hurwitz class numbers appearing in our Eichler--Selberg trace formula are defined in terms of class numbers of quartic CM fields. For applications of this Eichler--Selberg trace formula, we study the distribution of the generalized Hurwitz class numbers, prove class number relations, and carry out numerical computations of traces of Hecke operators for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{5})$ and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{29})$.

</details>


### [114] [Genuine and strongly genuine polynomials: With an application to the persistence of Galois groups under specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01969)
*Dante Bonolis,Lillian B. Pierce,Katharine Woo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发展了强n-本原多项式和n-本原多项式的理论，给出了它们分别在有理数域和有限域上特殊化后不可约或完全分裂的定量控制性质，并证明了四种等价刻画。作为应用，利用这些多项式估计了任意多项式在整数点上特殊化时伽罗瓦群保持不变的个数上界，结果同时推广到任意数域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了定量控制多元多项式在特殊化后保持可约性、完全分裂性以及伽罗瓦群不变性的特殊化个数，需要建立更精细的本原性理论。

Method: 定义了强n-本原多项式（控制不可约性）和n-本原多项式（控制完全分裂性），分别给出了四种等价刻画，并利用这些性质推导特殊化后伽罗瓦群同构的上界估计。

Result: 为两类多项式建立了四种等价刻画；证明了任意多项式在整数点上特殊化后，其分裂域伽罗瓦群与原有理函数域上伽罗瓦群不同构的特殊化个数有上界；结果在任意数域上同时成立。

Conclusion: 本原多项式理论能有效控制特殊化后的代数性质变化，为希尔伯特不可约性定理等经典结果提供了定量加强。

Abstract: We develop the theory of strongly $n$-genuine polynomials $F(Y,X_1,\ldots,X_n)$, which have the property that the number of specializations $F(Y,X_1,\mathbf{x}')$ with $\mathbf{x}'=(x_2,\ldots,x_n) \in \mathbb{Z}^{n-1}$ (respectively $\mathbf{x}' \in \mathbb{F}_p^{n-1}$) such that $F(Y,X_1,\mathbf{x}')$ is reducible over $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$ (respectively over $\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$) can be well-controlled quantitatively. We also develop the theory of a larger class of $n$-genuine polynomials $F(Y,X_1,\ldots,X_n)$, which have the property that the number of specializations $F(Y,X_1,\mathbf{x}')$ with $\mathbf{x}' \in \mathbb{Z}^{n-1}$ (respectively $\mathbf{x}' \in \mathbb{F}_p^{n-1}$) such that $F(Y,X_1,\mathbf{x}')$ splits completely over $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$ (respectively over $\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$) into factors that are linear in $Y$ can be well-controlled quantitatively. For each of these classes, we prove that there are four equivalent characterizations. As an application, we demonstrate that $n$-genuine and strongly $n$-genuine polynomials can be used to prove, for any polynomial $F(Y,X_1,\ldots,X_n)$, an upper bound for the number of specializations $F(Y,\mathbf{x})$ with $\mathbf{x}=(x_1,\ldots,x_n) \in \mathbb{Z}^n$ such that the Galois group of the splitting field of $F(Y,\mathbf{x})$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is not isomorphic to the Galois group of the splitting field of $F(Y,X_1,\ldots,X_n)$ over $\mathbb{Q}(X_1,\ldots,X_n)$. We simultaneously prove analogous results over any number field.

</details>


### [115] [Iwasawa-Type Spectral Resultant Growth Laws for Grover Walks on Graph Towers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02011)
*Jirô Akahori,Taro Hayashi,Ryoichi Suzuki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb Z_p^d$-图塔的Grover转移矩阵的Iwasawa型$p$-进增长律，通过谱结式统一处理多项式谱量$\det P(U_n)$，给出其$p$-进赋值的渐近公式，并建立了与Fitting理想、扭模、Kida公式及本征值零点对应的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Iwasawa理论的思想应用于图塔的Grover量子游走谱，建立多项式谱量的$p$-进增长律，统一并推广已有的固定非本征值公式。

Method: 定义通用Grover-Ihara谱多项式$\mathcal F_X(A,T)$及谱结式$\mathcal R_{X,P}(T)$，利用Iwasawa代数上的有限模的Fitting理想刻画其性质，通过Cuoco-Monsky型方法分析$v_p(\det P(U_n))$的渐近行为，并借助等变分解建立Kida公式。

Result: 当$P$与Bass因子互素且$\mathcal R_{X,P}$在扭特征处非零时，$\det P(U_n)$非零且其$p$-进赋值具有显式渐近公式，前导项由$\mathcal R_{X,P}$的$\mu$-和$\lambda$-不变量以及Bass因子修正项给出。对$P(A)=A-a$（$a\neq\pm1$且非本征值）恢复已有公式。此外，建立了$p$-群覆盖下谱结式的等变分解，导出非分歧等变Kida公式，并证明$\mathcal R_{X,P}$的扭零点与$P$的根作为Grover本征值出现一一对应。

Conclusion: 该工作系统建立了图塔Grover谱的Iwasawa理论框架，为量子游走的谱渐近分析提供了代数数论工具，并通过具体例子展示了理论的应用。

Abstract: Let $X_0\leftarrow X_1\leftarrow\cdots$ be a $\mathbb Z_p^d$-tower of finite graphs, and let $U_n$ be the Grover transition matrix on $X_n$. We study Iwasawa-type $p$-adic growth laws for the polynomial spectral quantities \[ \det P(U_n), \] where $P(A)$ is a monic polynomial. The basic object is the spectral resultant \[ \mathcal R_{X,P}(T)=\operatorname{Res}_A(\mathcal F_X(A,T),P(A)), \] where $\mathcal F_X(A,T)$ is the universal Grover--Ihara spectral polynomial of the tower. In the integral setting, this resultant generates the zeroth Fitting ideal of a natural finite module over the Iwasawa algebra; when the resultant is nonzero, this module is torsion. The polynomial $P$ packages prescribed spectral values into a single spectral packet. If $P$ is coprime to the Bass factor $A^2-1$ and $\mathcal R_{X,P}$ does not vanish at torsion characters, then $\det P(U_n)$ is nonzero for all $n$ and we prove a Cuoco--Monsky type leading asymptotic formula for $v_p(\det P(U_n))$. The leading terms are given explicitly by the $μ$- and $λ$-invariants of $\mathcal R_{X,P}$, with a separate correction coming from the Bass factor. For $P(A)=A-a$, with $a\ne\pm1$ and $a$ not an eigenvalue at any level, this recovers the leading invariants in the fixed non-eigenvalue formula for Grover characteristic polynomials.
  We also prove an equivariant factorization of spectral resultants for finite connected $p$-group covers. As a consequence, we obtain an unramified equivariant Kida formula under explicit integrality and nonzero-resultant assumptions. Finally, when $\gcd(P,A^2-1)=1$, we show that torsion zeros of $\mathcal R_{X,P}$ correspond exactly to occurrences of roots of $P$ as Grover eigenvalues at finite levels. The examples include the $K_3$-tower, non-abelian Heisenberg $5$-group covers, and an explicit torsion-zero spectral packet.

</details>


### [116] [Algorithms for hyperelliptic Mumford Curves $p$-adic Uniformization, $p$-adic integrals and $p$-adic heights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02160)
*Enis Kaya,Marc Masdeu,J. Steffen Müller,Marius van der Put*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了计算超椭圆Mumford曲线的p-adic Schottky群、Abelian积分和Schneider高度的算法，并基于Kadziela逼近定理的推广给出了数值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mumford曲线作为椭圆曲线Tate均匀化的推广，提供了Riemann曲面均匀化的p-adic类比，但超椭圆Mumford曲线的具体算法尚不完善，需要系统性的计算方法。

Method: 基于对Kadziela逼近定理的推广，计算均匀化超椭圆Mumford曲线的p-adic Schottky群W；利用该均匀化计算p-adic Abelian积分；利用Werner公式（用theta函数表示Schneider高度的p部分）计算p-adic Schneider高度。

Result: 给出了超椭圆Mumford曲线均匀化及相关不变量（Abelian积分、Schneider高度）的可行算法，并在SageMath中实现了数值示例。

Conclusion: 本文填补了超椭圆Mumford曲线计算算法方面的空白，为p-adic算术几何中的实际计算提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Mumford curves generalize the Tate uniformization of elliptic curves with split multiplicative reduction and provide p-adic analogues of the uniformization of Riemann surfaces. In this paper, we present several algorithms for hyperelliptic Mumford curves. For a given hyperelliptic Mumford curve $X$ defined over a finite extension of the field of p-adic numbers for some $p\neq 2$, we first describe how to compute a p-adic Schottky group W that uniformizes X; this is based on our extension to Kadziela's approximation theorem. As applications, we explain how to use this uniformization in order to compute p-adic Abelian integrals and $p$-adic Schneider heights on X; the latter uses Werner's formula expressing the p-part of the Schneider height in terms of theta functions. We illustrate our algorithms with numerical examples computed using the computer algebra system SageMath.

</details>


### [117] [The trianguline variety for reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02215)
*Andrea Conti,Mohamed Moakher,Julian Quast*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了分裂连通约化群的三角线簇，推广了Breuil、Hellmann和Schraen关于其局部结构的定理，建立了在三角化参数的各种正则性条件所决定区域上的光滑性，以及在这些光滑区域外某些点的正规性。同时，证明了带G结构的(φ,Γ_K)-模的结晶性判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Breuil、Hellmann和Schraen关于三角线簇局部结构的经典定理，深入理解分裂连通约化群的三角线簇的几何性质。

Method: 利用三角化参数的正则性条件划分区域，研究三角线簇在这些区域上的光滑性；同时通过证明带G结构的(φ,Γ_K)-模的结晶性判别准则来辅助建立正规性结论。

Result: 在三角化参数的各种正则性条件所决定的区域上建立了三角线簇的光滑性，并在光滑区域外的某些点证明了正规性。

Conclusion: 成功推广了已知定理，深化了对分裂连通约化群三角线簇局部结构的认识。

Abstract: We study the trianguline variety for split connected reductive groups. We generalize a theorem of Breuil, Hellmann, and Schraen about its local structure, establishing smoothness over the loci determined by various regularity conditions on the triangulation parameter, and normality at certain points outside of these smooth loci. Along the way, we prove a crystallinity criterion for $(\varphi,Γ_K)$-modules with $\mathsf G$-structure.

</details>


### [118] [Cyclic Codes and Cyclically Covering Subspaces over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02239)
*Yangcheng Li,Pingzhi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究循环覆盖子空间（其所有循环移位的并集覆盖整个空间）的最大余维数 \(h_q(n)\)，通过循环码理论建立了该问题与循环码中全重量码字存在性、最大重量以及无全重量码字循环码计数之间的联系，并给出了有限域上的精确结果与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环覆盖子空间的最大余维数 \(h_q(n)\) 是一个组合/编码理论问题，已有若干结果但缺乏系统性研究。本文旨在利用循环码这一代数工具，深入刻画 \(h_q(n)\) 与循环码结构之间的内在关系，并解决特定参数下 \(h_q(n)\) 的正性判断问题。

Method: 将循环覆盖子空间问题转化为循环码中是否存在全重量码字的问题；利用循环码的代数结构（如生成多项式、零化子等）分析最大重量；通过组合计数与代数方法研究无全重量码字循环码的数量。

Result: (1) \(h_q(n)=0\) 当且仅当每个非零循环码都含有全重量码字；(2) 当 \(h_q(n)>0\) 时，给出了无全重量码字循环码最大重量的紧界及达到该界的显式例子；(3) 完全确定了 \(\mathbb F_2\) 上无全重量码字的循环码个数，并给出 \(\mathbb F_3\) 上的下界；(4) 证明了对任意奇素数 \(q\ge 3\) 和整数 \(m\ge 4\)，有 \(h_q\left(\frac{q^m+1}{2}\right)>0\)。

Conclusion: 本文通过循环码理论系统研究了循环覆盖子空间，不仅揭示了 \(h_q(n)\) 与循环码深度性质的联系，还在多个方向上取得了定量结果，为进一步研究该问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let \(q\) be a power of a prime \(p\), and let \(n\) be a positive integer. A subspace \(U\subseteq \mathbb F_q^n\) is called cyclically covering if the union of all its cyclic shifts covers \(\mathbb F_q^n\), and \(h_q(n)\) denotes the maximum possible codimension of such a subspace. This paper studies cyclically covering subspaces via cyclic codes. We first prove that \(h_q(n)=0\) if and only if every nonzero cyclic code in \(\mathbb F_q^n\) contains a full-weight codeword. We also relate \(h_q(n)\) to the maximum weights of cyclic codes. In particular, when \(h_q(n)>0\), we obtain sharp bounds for the maximum weight of cyclic codes without full-weight codewords and provide explicit examples attaining these bounds. Moreover, we study the number of cyclic codes containing no full-weight codeword. We determine this number completely over \(\mathbb F_2\), and give lower bounds over \(\mathbb F_3\). From this, we prove that if \(q\ge 3\) is an odd prime and \(m\ge 4\) is an integer, then \(h_q\left(\frac{q^m+1}{2}\right)>0\).

</details>


### [119] [Beyond the Giampietro--Darmon Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02306)
*Michael A. Daas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Giampietro-Darmon关于p进交比CM点无穷乘积范数分解公式的适用范围，从亏格为0的Shimura曲线推广到其Atkin-Lehner商亏格为0的情形，通过解决Howard-Yang计数问题的p-逆版本实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Giampietro和Darmon猜想了一个关于p进交比CM点无穷乘积代数数范数的分解公式，该公式与Gross-Zagier分解高度类似，但原猜想仅适用于Shimura曲线亏格为0的情形，且大部分情形已被证明。本文旨在将该公式的成立范围扩展到更广的情形。

Method: 将分解公式的成立条件从Shimura曲线本身亏格为0放宽到其Atkin-Lehner商的亏格为0，并通过解决Howard-Yang此前研究的计数问题的p-逆版本来实现这一推广。

Result: 成功将Giampietro-Darmon分解公式的适用范围扩展到Shimura曲线的Atkin-Lehner商亏格为0的更多情形，超出了原始猜想所覆盖的范围。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入Atkin-Lehner商亏格条件并解决p-逆计数问题，显著推广了p进交比CM点无穷乘积的范数分解公式，深化了p进均匀化理论与Gross-Zagier型公式之间的联系。

Abstract: Giampietro and Darmon conjectured a formula for the norm of various algebraic numbers, obtained as infinite products of $p$-adic cross-ratios of CM points. These quantities arose from the $p$-adic uniformisation of Shimura curves and displayed strong parallels with the Gross--Zagier factorisation for the norms of the differences between two singular moduli. The conjectured formula was conditional on the genus of the Shimura curve being zero, and in earlier work, this formula was proved in most cases. In this work, we extend the validity of the factorisation formula beyond what was conjectured by Giampietro and Darmon to many more cases, by relating this to the genus of an Atkin--Lehner quotient of the Shimura curve being zero instead. To this end, we solve a $p$-inverted version of a counting problem that was previously considered in work of Howard and Yang.

</details>


### [120] [The Gauss periods and cyclotomic matrices involving Gauss sums over cyclic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02392)
*Hai-Liang Wu,Li-Yuan Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Gauss周期的算术性质和循环群上的特征和，研究了以Gauss和为元素的循环矩阵$A_k(χ)$，其中$N=p^m$为素数幂，$k$是$\varphi(N)$的因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Gauss和构成的循环矩阵的结构与性质，利用Gauss周期和特征和的算术工具进行理论分析。

Method: 利用Gauss周期的算术性质以及循环群上的特征和，对形如$A_k(χ)=[G_N(χ^{ki+kj})]$的循环矩阵进行数学分析。

Result: 论文给出了该循环矩阵的相关算术性质和结构特征。

Conclusion: 通过Gauss周期和特征和的方法，成功揭示了素数幂模$N=p^m$下Gauss和循环矩阵的数学性质。

Abstract: In this paper, by using the arithmetic properties of the Gauss periods and character sums over cyclic groups, we study the cyclotomic matrix
  $$A_k(χ)=\left[G_N(χ^{ki+ki})\right]_{0\le i,j\le \varphi(N)/k-1},$$
  where $N=p^m$ is a prime power, $\varphi(\cdot)$ is the Euler totient function, $k$ is a divisor of $\varphi(N)$, $χ$ is a generator of character group $\widehat{(\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z})^{\times}}$, and
  $$G_N(χ^{ki+kj})=\sum_{x\in\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z}}χ^{ki+kj}(x)e^{2πix/N}$$
  is the Gauss sum over $\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z}$.

</details>
