### [121] [Pulsar Science with the SKAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01288)
*Bhal Chandra Joshi,Aris Karastergiou,Marta Burgay*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文概述了SKAO望远镜（SKA-Low和SKA-Mid）凭借其大瞬时灵敏度、宽频率覆盖和灵活观测模式等特性，将在脉冲星科学领域带来革命性影响。全书11章分别阐述了这些能力对不同脉冲星科学领域的作用，包括新深度巡天将发现数千颗新脉冲星，为研究中子星物理及其环境奠定基础，进而推动基础物理学研究（如检验相对论引力、研究纳赫兹引力波和核物质状态方程），并强调了与其他科学案例的协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SKAO望远镜具备大瞬时灵敏度、宽频率覆盖和灵活观测模式等卓越能力，有望彻底改变射电天文学尤其是脉冲星天文学的面貌，需要系统评估其在脉冲星科学各领域的潜在影响。

Method: 本文采用综述方式，对书中11章内容进行简要总结，梳理不同脉冲星科学用例之间的相互关系，包括新深度巡天（覆盖银河系场、球状星团和银心）发现新脉冲星，以及这些发现如何支撑中子星物理和环境研究，最终服务于基础物理学检验。

Result: 新深度巡天将发现数千颗新脉冲星，为研究中子星物理及其环境提供支撑，进而推动检验相对论引力、研究纳赫兹引力波和核物质状态方程等基础物理学贡献。

Conclusion: SKAO望远镜将成为脉冲星天文学的变革性工具，通过大规模脉冲星发现和精准测量，在基础物理学领域取得突破性进展，同时与其他科学案例存在广泛协同效应。

Abstract: The large instantaneous sensitivity, wide frequency coverage and flexible observation modes, with large number of beams in the sky, are the main features of the SKA observatory's two telescopes, the SKA-Low and the SKA-Mid. Owing to these capabilities, the SKAO telescopes are going to be a game-changer for radio astronomy in general and pulsar astronomy in particular. Eleven chapters in this book describe their impact on different areas of pulsar science. In this overview article each chapter is briefly summarised and the inter-relationship between different pulsar science use cases are explored: new deep surveys, covering the Galactic field, globular clusters and the Galactic centre, will discover thousands of new pulsars; these will form the backbone for studies of neutron star physics and of their environments. The enhanced understanding provided by these studies will feed into the main contributions to fundamental physics from pulsar astronomy: testing relativistic gravity, studying gravitational waves in the nano-Hz regime and studying the equation of state of nuclear matter. Synergies with other science cases are also highlighted throughout this overview.

</details>


### [122] [A possible high-redshift origin for the short GRB 061201: implications of a compact binary merger beyond cosmic noon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01337)
*E. Troja,B. O'Connor,Y. -H. Yang,T. M. Gaudin,M. Yadav,N. Passaleva,S. Dichiara*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了短伽马射线暴GRB 061201，提出该事件实际上发生在红移z>2的暗淡星系（F322W2~28.4 AB星等）中，而非此前认为的近邻星系。如果这一解释成立，GRB 061201将成为已知最遥远的短伽马暴之一，将致密双星并合事件追溯至宇宙仅约20亿年的早期时代，对理解早期宇宙中的r过程元素产生具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移（z>2）短伽马暴极为罕见，但它们是追踪早期宇宙致密双星并合事件以及理解r过程元素产生的关键。GRB 061201是一个存在争议的特殊事件，其光学余辉虽被精确定位，但未能确认宿主星系，此前提出的近邻星系关联都需要解释大空间分离。因此需要重新审视该事件的真实起源。

Method: 结合余辉观测约束和来自JWST的深近红外成像数据，对GRB 061201的可能宿主星系进行重新分析，通过比较不同红移假设下观测特征的一致性，论证高红移起源的合理性。

Result: 研究发现，GRB 061201的观测特征更自然地解释为发生在一个红移z>2的暗淡星系（F322W2~28.4 AB星等）内，而非此前认为的近邻星系。该高红移起源能连贯地解释暴发的全部观测现象。

Conclusion: 如果这一解释得到确认，GRB 061201将成为已知最遥远的短伽马暴之一，将致密双星并合事件的观测记录扩展到宇宙年龄仅约20亿年的时期，为早期宇宙中r过程元素产生和致密双星并合的研究提供了重要样本。

Abstract: Short gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) at redshift z>2 remain exceptionally rare, yet they are crucial for tracing compact binary mergers in the early Universe and understanding their role in the production of r-process elements. GRB 061201 is an unusual and still debated event: although its optical afterglow was accurately localized, no secure coincident host galaxy was identified, and the proposed associations with nearby galaxies all require a large separation between the GRB and its birth site. In this work, we revisit GRB 061201 and argue that the observations are more naturally explained if the burst occurred within a faint F322W2~28.4 AB mag galaxy at z>2. By combining constraints from the afterglow and deep near-infrared imaging from JWST, we show that a distant origin provides a coherent explanation of the burst phenomenology. If confirmed, GRB 061201 would represent one of the most distant short GRBs known, extending the observed compact merger population to an epoch when the Universe was only about two billion years old.

</details>


### [123] [X-ray polarization in magnetized neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01357)
*Tanuman Ghosh,Shiv Sethi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过IXPE偏振观测，研究强磁化中子星（磁星和普通脉冲星）中光子散射产生的X射线偏振特性，发现磁星通常具有更高的线偏振度，但单一散射模型无法完全解释观测到的磁星偏振谱形，暗示需要多成分模型。同时分析了真空双折射效应的影响，并对未来更宽能段偏振探测做出预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IXPE偏振探测为理解磁化致密天体物理打开了新窗口，但现有理论模型对偏振特性与磁场、入射光性质关系的理解尚不充分，特别是磁星与普通脉冲星偏振差异的物理根源、以及真空双折射效应的影响机制需要系统研究。

Method: 基于强磁化系统中光子散射产生偏振的主导范式，系统研究散射光的偏振对入射光几何构型、偏振态以及磁场强度的依赖关系，分析真空双折射效应对光子偏振的影响，并计算宽磁场范围下偏振光的谱形特征。

Result: 磁星通常比普通脉冲星具有更高的线偏振度，与IXPE观测一致；在特定条件下，模型有助于理解普通脉冲星和弱磁化中子星的偏振度及其谱依赖关系；但单一散射成分模型无法解释磁星观测偏振的谱形，暗示系统更为复杂（如多成分）。

Conclusion: 强磁场散射模型可部分解释IXPE观测的中子星偏振特征，但磁星偏振谱形需要多成分模型；未来更宽能段的X射线偏振探测器可验证本工作关于共振频率附近偏振光谱形状的预测。

Abstract: X-ray polarimetry has opened a new window into understanding the physics around magnetized compact objects. IXPE detection of linear polarization from such systems has prompted a new spurt of theoretical modeling. Our study is based on the dominant paradigm that the observed polarization arises from the scattering of photons around highly magnetized systems. Our main focus is the dependence of the polarization of the scattered light on properties of the incoming light, i.e., geometry and the polarization state, and the determination of the spectral shape of the polarized light for a wide range of magnetic field strengths. We also analyze the impact of vacuum birefringence on photon polarization. We show that, generically, we expect a higher linear degree of polarization from magnetars as compared to normal pulsars, which is in agreement with IXPE observations. Under some conditions, our study helps to understand the observed degree of polarization from normal pulsars and low-magnetized neutron stars and their spectral dependence. However, we cannot conclusively explain the spectral shape of the observed polarization for magnetars using only a single component emission from scattering in a strong magnetic field. This probably points to the system being more complex, e.g., multi-component, than our study allows for. Upcoming X-ray polarimeters with broader energy coverage could probe some of our other predictions, e.g., the spectral shape of the polarized light close to the resonance frequency.

</details>


### [124] [Constraining the near-source relativistic wind medium using Fast Radio Burst circular polarization data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01369)
*Om Gupta,Pawan Kumar,Paz Beniamini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究快速射电暴（FRB）中圆偏振（Stokes V）的起源，提出法拉第转换机制（磁陀星星风中的近源区域）可以解释观测到的圆偏振特征，而非源自FRB源本身。模型考虑了FRB波对正负电子对有效质量的影响，能解释圆偏振的广泛变化及非探测上限，并首次通过偏振数据约束FRB相关致密天体的星风参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB表现出多样的频谱-时间特征，通过斯托克斯偏振测量可以探测传播路径和源物理。本文旨在探究观测到的圆偏振是否由磁陀星星风中的法拉第转换产生，而非源内禀属性，从而揭示FRB环境物理。

Method: 计算了FRB波存在下正负电子对有效质量的增加，并基于法拉第转换模型分析磁陀星星风中的圆偏振产生机制。利用观测上限约束星风光度、磁化强度、体洛伦兹因子及离子存在时的有效粒子质量，并通过频率分辨的斯托克斯谱估计星风环境。

Result: 法拉第转换模型可以解释FRB中观测到的广泛圆偏振范围及其频繁的非探测。高星风/低FRB光度区域中斯托克斯参数随频率快速振荡导致圆偏振消偏。显著低于典型FRB光度的暴也可以产生可测量的圆偏振。模型适用于多个源（FRB 20201124A、FRB 20180301A、SGR 1935+2154），约束了瞬时星风参数。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次通过偏振数据推断与FRB相关的致密天体星风性质，证明了法拉第转换在磁陀星星风中产生圆偏振的可行性，为理解FRB环境及传播效应提供了新手段。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) exhibit diverse spectro-temporal characteristics, which can probe vital propagation and source physics via Stokes polarimetry. We investigate whether the circular polarization (Stokes $V$) observed in some bursts is produced by Faraday conversion in the near-source wind of magnetars rather than being intrinsic to the source. Our calculation includes the increase in the effective mass of $e^\pm$ in the presence of the FRB wave. We find that Faraday conversion in the magnetar wind can explain the broad range of observed circular polarization in FRBs, including its frequent non-detection. Observationally derived upper limits on $V$ provide stringent constraints on the wind luminosity, magnetization, bulk Lorentz factor, and effective particle mass when ions are present. When available, frequency resolved Stokes spectra offer direct estimates of the wind environment. The Stokes parameters can undergo rapid oscillations with frequency in the high-wind/low-FRB-luminosity regime, resulting in Stokes-V depolarization. Bursts with significantly lower luminosities than typical FRBs can also develop measurable circular polarization, within the model framework. Additionally, separate zones are favored for significant circular polarization and rotation measure, when the model is applicable. The model constrains instantaneous wind parameters for several sources, including FRB 20201124A, FRB 20180301A, and SGR 1935+2154. This work represents the first instance in which properties of winds from compact objects associated with FRBs are inferred from polarization data.

</details>


### [125] [AI-enabled gravitational-waves searches for binary neutron stars at optimal sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01372)
*Bhavya Gupta,Deep Chatterjee,William Benoit,Ethan Marx,Christina Reissel,Seiya Tsukamoto,Kyungseop Yoon,Michael W. Coughlin,Philip Harris,Erik Katsavounidis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Aframe——首个在LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA实时观测中成功部署的AI引力波搜索算法，通过外差处理将双中子星信号搜索的灵敏度提升至与匹配滤波管道相当的水平，同时显著降低计算成本和延迟，仅需单张非旗舰GPU即可在线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时搜索双中子星并合引力波信号面临巨大计算挑战——需将流式数据与约百万个参考波形模板匹配，这需要上千个CPU核心。传统匹配滤波方法计算成本高昂，亟需更高效的替代方案以实现低延迟、低成本的在线搜索。

Method: 采用神经网络学习数据中是否存在信号特征。针对双中子星信号持续时间长的特点，引入外差（heterodyning）处理降低数据频率，使原本用于双黑洞搜索的网络架构可直接适用。采用推理即服务（inference-as-a-service）工具实现分布式GPU资源的离线分析。

Result: Aframe在LVK第四次观测运行中成功部署，是首个实时发现多个双黑洞的AI搜索算法。本文证明该方法可扩展至更低质量的双中子星区域，灵敏度与匹配滤波管道相当，且计算成本和延迟更低。在线部署仅需单张非旗舰GPU。

Conclusion: Aframe证明了AI搜索在引力波天文学中的实用价值：不仅适用于实时在线分析，还可通过推理即服务工具高效进行归档数据离线分析，为未来多信使天文学提供了一种计算高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Gravitational Waves (GWs) represent the newest window of astronomy, furthering our understanding of compact objects like black holes and neutron stars in the Universe. The signal from two merging neutron stars is especially interesting since it brings the prospect of concordant electromagnetic and neutrino emissions. Such multi-messenger observations have a transformational impact on fundamental physics, nuclear matter, astrophysics, and gravity. It was first witnessed in 2017 with the detection of the binary neutron star (BNS) merger GW170817. However, searching for BNS signals in real-time in the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) GW detectors presents a computational challenge, as the data streaming out must be matched against $\sim$ million reference waveforms, which requires up to a thousand CPU cores. We present a different approach using neural networks to learn the presence of a signal in the data. Our algorithm, called Aframe, was deployed in the LVK's fourth observing run and was the first artificial intelligence (AI)-enabled search to detect multiple binary black holes (BBHs) live. In this work, we demonstrate that the approach extends to the lower-mass BNS regime, and is the first AI-enabled search that achieves sensitivity comparable to matched-filter pipelines at lower computational and latency costs. The challenge of the longer-duration BNS signals is addressed by heterodyning the data, following which the network architecture used for BBHs is sufficient to distinguish signal versus background. We also show that this analysis requires a single non-flagship GPU for online deployment. Furthermore, the design and adoption of inference-as-a-service tools allow rapid offline analysis using a distributed pool of GPU resources. Hence, aside from the use case of rapid online data analysis, we also establish the use of Aframe for efficient archival data analysis.

</details>


### [126] [Magneto-rotational instabilities in solids: application to neutron-star crusts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01505)
*Arthur G. Suvorov,Thomas Celora,Kostas D. Kokkotas*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁旋转不稳定性在固体介质中的可行性，发现只有当剪切足够强（磁张力超过固体剪切模量）时才能发生，应用于双中子星合并场景表明只有当自转频率≥300Hz时，磁场的增长才能在合并前的星壳中发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁旋转不稳定性在流体（分层、扩散等）中已被广泛研究，但尚未探究其是否能在固体中运作。行星或简并星内部的固态区域可能相对于液态/气态层存在差速旋转，因此需要研究弹性如何抑制该不稳定性。

Method: 采用简化、平面平行分析，比较磁张力（液态中不稳定产生的）与固体剪切模量，判断弹性介质中磁增长的条件。

Result: 只有当流动剪切足够强，使得液态中磁旋转不稳定性产生的磁张力超过弹性腔体的剪切模量时，磁增长才能在固体中发生。应用于双中子星合并：仅当自转频率≥300 Hz时，合并前星壳中磁场可被放大；若粘性加热在共振前弱化晶体结构，所需自转频率可降低。

Conclusion: 磁旋转不稳定性在固体中受到弹性抑制，仅在极强剪切条件下可行；该结果对双中子星并合前磁场的放大条件给出了定量约束。

Abstract: The magneto-rotational instability can generate strong, turbulent substructure within magnetised shear flows. The efficacy of the mechanism as a function of microphysical aspects of the fluid, such as stratification and diffusivity, has been explored extensively. One aspect that has not been studied thus far, however, is whether the instability can also operate in solids. Motivated by the possibility that solid regions within planets or degenerate stars may rotate differentially with respect to liquid or gaseous layers during some phase of their life, we examine the extent to which elasticity suppresses the instability. A simplified, plane-parallel analysis reveals that only in cases where the flow is strongly sheared, such that the magnetic tension that would result from the instability in a liquid exceeds the shear modulus of the elastic cavity, can magnetic growth occur. In the context of dynamical tides in binary neutron-star mergers, this implies that the magnetic field can be amplified in the crust prior to coalescence only if the star boasts a spin frequency of $\gtrsim 300$Hz. If viscous heating weakens the crystalline structure prior to resonance, the required spin frequency is reduced.

</details>


### [127] [Signatures of Two Distinct Epochs of FRB 20240114A from January to August 2024 Based on its Energy and Waiting Time Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01576)
*Xiao Li,Ying Gu,En-Wei Liang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文分析了FRB 20240114A在2024年1月28日至8月29日期间由FAST探测到的爆发能量和等待时间分布。全样本无法用简单幂律、弯曲幂律、阈值幂律或Band函数拟合；而单日爆发数>50的子样本可用弯曲幂律或阈值幂律拟合，等待时间分布更适合Weibull模型。以2024年3月21日为界分为两个时期，两时期在弯曲幂律参数β、爆发率参数r、高能段能量分布指数及等待时间中位数上存在显著差异，表明两个时期可能由不同类型的爆发主导，可能与辐射区物理性质变化有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对快速射电暴FRB 20240114A的长时间（约7个月）高灵敏度观测数据，系统分析其爆发能量分布和等待时间分布，探究是否存在不同爆发类型或物理机制变化的证据。

Method: 利用FAST望远镜在2024年1月28日至8月29日期间对FRB 20240114A的观测数据，对全样本和子样本（单日爆发数>50）分别进行能量分布拟合（简单幂律、弯曲幂律、阈值幂律、Band函数）和等待时间分布拟合（Poisson模型、Weibull模型），并以2024年3月21日为界分两时期对比分析。

Result: 全样本能量分布无法用四种模型拟合，等待时间分布（剔除<0.5s间隔后）无法用Poisson或Weibull拟合。子样本能量分布可用弯曲幂律或阈值幂律拟合，等待时间分布更适合Weibull模型。两时期的弯曲幂律参数β平均值分别为1.006±0.074和1.236±0.183；后期子样本的Weibull爆发率参数r更小；高能段（>6×10^37 erg）能量分布指数分别为-1.97和-2.34；后期等待时间中位数更大。

Conclusion: FRB 20240114A的两个时期可能由不同类型的爆发主导，推测与辐射区物理性质的变化有关。

Abstract: A comprehensive analysis of the energy and waiting time distributions of the bursts from FRB 20240114A detected by the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical Radio Telescope between 28 January and 29 August 2024 is presented. For the full sample, its energy distribution cannot be fitted with the simple power-law (SPL),bent power-law (BPL), thresholded power-law (TPL) or Band function models, and its waiting time distribution excluding intervals shorter than 0.5 s cannot be fitted with the Poisson or Weibull models. Nevertheless, for the subsamples with more than 50 bursts in single-day observations, their energy distributions can be fitted with the BPL or TPL models, and their waiting time distributions are better described by a Weibull model. It is noted that the best-fitting BPL parameter $β$ is approximately invariant within the epochs before and after 21 March 2024, with an average of $\bar β_b = 1.006 \pm 0.074$ and $\bar β_a = 1.236 \pm 0.183$ (one standard deviation), respectively. Most subsamples from the later epoch have a smaller burst rate parameter $r$ in the Weibull model than those from the earlier epoch. The majority of bursts with $E>10^{39}$ erg occurred in the earlier epoch. The energy distributions in the high-energy range ($> 6\times10^{37}$ erg) differ significantly between the two epochs, and power-law fits to $dN/dE$ yield indices of $-1.97_{-0.02}^{+0.02}$ and $-2.34_{-0.06}^{+0.06}$, respectively. The median of the waiting time distribution of the later epoch is larger than that in the earlier epoch. These results suggest that the two epochs may be dominated by different types of bursts, possibly attributed to changes in the physical properties of the emission region.

</details>


### [128] [Bolometric correction factor and radiative efficiency for the super-Eddington accretion flow in tidal disruption events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01604)
*Yongxin Wu,Erlin Qiao,Xuan Fang,Yiyang Lin,Jifeng Liu,Meng Guo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过辐射流体动力学模拟和后处理谱计算，研究了潮汐瓦解事件中早期超爱丁顿吸积流的辐射效率与各向同性等效X射线辐射修正因子，发现两者均依赖于黑洞质量与观测角度，并将结果应用于具体TDE事件以缓解“能量缺失问题”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算TDE中早期超爱丁顿吸积流的辐射效率和辐射修正因子，是理解超大质量黑洞周围吸积流性质的关键，特别是为了解决TDE中存在的“能量缺失问题”。

Method: 首先进行TDE环境下的超爱丁顿吸积流辐射流体动力学模拟，然后采用后处理方法对模拟数据计算涌现谱，基于涌现谱得到各向同性等效X射线辐射修正因子k_bol和辐射效率η。

Result: k_bol和η均依赖于黑洞质量和观测角度；k_bol范围约为数十至数千，η范围约为10^{-3}-10^{-1}（黑洞质量10^{6-7}M⊙，观测角度0°-90°）。

Conclusion: 将所得k_bol和η应用于具体TDE事件可显著缓解所谓的“能量缺失问题”。

Abstract: The estimate of the bolometric luminosity and the radiative efficiency are two key aspects for understanding the properties of the accretion flow around a supermassive black hole (BH). In this paper, we focus on the estimate of the bolometric luminosity and the radiative efficiency of the early super-Eddington accretion flow in tidal disruption events (TDEs). Specifically, we first perform radiation hydrodynamic simulations of super-Eddington accretion flow in TDE environment, and then calculate the corresponding emergent spectra with the method of post processing for the simulation data. Based on the emergent spectra, we calculate the isotropic-equivalent X-ray bolometric correction factor $k_\mathrm{bol}$ and the radiative efficiency $η$ of the super-Eddington accretion flow. We find that both $k_\mathrm{bol}$ and $η$ are BH mass and viewing-angle dependent. $k_\mathrm{bol}$ is in the range of about a few tens to a few thousands, and $η$ is in the range of $\sim 10^{-3}-10^{-1}$ for BH mass in the range of $10^{6-7}M_\odot$ and the viewing angle in the range of $0^{\rm o}-90^{\rm o}$. Finally, we apply the derived $k_\mathrm{bol}$ and $η$ to some specific TDEs to estimate the accreted mass during an event, which can significantly alleviate the so-called missing energy problem in TDEs.

</details>


### [129] [X-ray Fourier lag-frequency spectra modulated by stochastic turbulent acceleration in the jets of high-frequency-peaked BL Lac](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01606)
*Guang-Cheng Xiao,Wen Hu,Da-Guo Jiang,Jun-Xian Wang,Zhen-Yi Cai,Da-Hai Yan,Fang-Wu Lu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用含有随机湍流加速（STA）的单区轻子模型，理论研究了HBL天体X射线波段的时间延迟谱，发现STA与辐射冷却、逃逸过程的竞争产生了硬延迟、软延迟及过渡态三种时延模式，且SSC冷却可放大时延信号，为解释HBL X射线耀发中的多样化时延特征提供了统一量化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线波段的时间延迟是喷流物理的关键诊断工具，且在HBL天体中频繁被观测到，但缺乏统一的理论解释框架。本文旨在从理论上阐明时延特征的形成机制。

Method: 采用包含随机湍流加速（STA）的单区轻子模型，通过计算Fourier时延-频率谱，系统分析STA、辐射冷却和逃逸过程三者竞争对时延特征的影响。

Result: 1）模型产生了硬/正延迟、软/负延迟以及过渡态三种时延模式；2）STA通过抑制高能电子冷却放大了过渡态和软延迟区域的时延信号；3）非线性的同步自康普顿（SSC）冷却进一步增强了时延的涌现；4）模型可解释TeV亮耀发中的大时延以及时延幅度与耀发时标的正相关关系。

Conclusion: 该模型为解释HBL天体X射线耀发中观测到的多样化时延特征提供了统一的理论框架，SSC冷却效应可以解释大时延及耀发时标与时延幅度的相关性。

Abstract: X-ray interband time lags are key diagnostics of jet physics and are frequently detected in high-frequency peaked BL Lac (HBL) objects at different epochs with various X-ray telescopes. In this work, we theoretically investigate Fourier lag-frequency spectra using a generic one-zone leptonic model incorporating the stochastic turbulent acceleration (STA), which plays a crucial role in shaping the emitted photon spectra. We demonstrate that the competition between STA, radiative cooling, and escape processes not only gives rise to two well-defined time-lag regimes: hard/positive and soft/negative lags, but also reveals the existence of a transition between the two regimes. Our results indicate that time lags in the transitional and soft-lag regimes can be clearly amplified and modified by STA's suppression of high-energy electron cooling, and nonlinear synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) cooling can further amplify the emergence of time lags. We conclude that the adopted model offers a unifying quantitative framework for interpreting the diverse time-lag signatures observed in the X-ray flares of HBLs. Additionally, SSC cooling effects can account for the relatively large lags observed in TeV-bright flares, as well as the observed trend between lag amplitude and flare duration: the larger the flare duration, the larger the lag.

</details>


### [130] [Polarization Architecture of Steady GRMHD Jets from the Horizon to Infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01820)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Yehui Hou,Yu Song,Yosuke Mizuno,Bin Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个半解析框架，用于生成从近水平区域到约10^5 r_g的静止、轴对称GRMHD喷流的极化图像，并发现极化诊断中存在新的尺度依赖性分离：在光子环外，等离子体负载通过惯性驱动的磁场缠绕改变极化角轮廓；在大图像平面半径处，极化角随半径呈幂律分布，指数由喷流准直轮廓决定；而在近水平区域，喷流收敛于仅由黑洞自旋控制的普适极化模式。这种收敛是分层的，使得不同效应得以解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速探索引力和磁流体动力学流如何将尺度依赖的特征印记在喷流形态和极化上，并建立连接GRMHD喷流动力学与分辨图像结构的近消色差极化诊断方法。

Method: 开发了一个半解析框架，用于静止、轴对称GRMHD喷流的极化图像生成，覆盖从近水平区域到约10^5 r_g的广域参数空间。

Result: 识别出极化诊断中新的尺度依赖性分离：光子环外等离子体负载改变极化角轮廓；大半径处极化角呈幂律分布，指数由喷流准直轮廓决定；近水平区域喷流收敛于仅由黑洞自旋控制的普适极化模式，且收敛是分层的。

Conclusion: 建立了连接GRMHD喷流动力学与分辨图像结构的近消色差极化诊断方法，对高分辨率偏振测量以及约束黑洞自旋和喷流形成具有直接意义。

Abstract: We develop a semi-analytic framework for stationary, axisymmetric GRMHD jets that efficiently generates resolved polarized images from the near-horizon region out to $\sim 10^5\,r_g$ across a broad parameter space, enabling rapid exploration of how gravity and magnetohydrodynamic flows imprint scale-dependent signatures on jet morphology and polarization. We identify a new scale-dependent separation in polarimetric diagnostics. Outside the photon ring, plasma loading strongly modifies the polarization-angle profile of the integrated jet-layer emission through inertia-driven winding of the magnetic field. At large image-plane radii, the polarization angle follows a power-law in radius, with an index determined by the jet collimation profile. Near the horizon, in contrast, jets converge to a universal polarization pattern controlled solely by black hole spin. This convergence is hierarchical: differences in velocity and magnetic-field structure are erased first, whereas collimation-dependent differences persist to smaller radii, thereby allowing these effects to be disentangled. These results establish a largely achromatic polarimetric diagnostic that connects GRMHD jet dynamics to resolved image structure, with direct implications for high-resolution polarimetry and for constraining black hole spin and jet formation.

</details>


### [131] [Colour evolution in the radio afterglow of GRB 241025A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01941)
*S. Giarratana,O. S. Salafia,L. Nava,G. Ghirlanda,M. Giroletti,S. Antier,M. Pillas,T. Hussenot-Desenonges,A. Iskandar,M. Tanasan,G. Oganesyan,N. Di Lalla,N. Omodei*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了GRB241025A余辉的多波段观测活动，发现标准余辉模型无法解释其射电波段颜色演化，通过引入光学深度增强因子τ_enh=500的结构化喷流前向激波模型可以较好地拟合数据，该增强可能源于下游未经历Fermi I加速的冷电子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB241025A被多个探测器（Swift、Einstein Probe、Fermi/GBM、SVOM、Konus-Wind、VZLUSAT-2）同时探测到，为系统研究其多波段余辉演化提供了独特机会。标准余辉模型在慢冷却机制下的预测与观测数据存在偏差，亟需理论修正来解释射电波段颜色演化。

Method: 开展了射电、近红外、光学和X射线多波段观测活动；采用半经验拟合描述余辉行为；引入半解析模型，对光学深度引入倍增因子τ_enh以人工提高同步自吸收频率ν_a；采用结构化喷流前向激波模型进行拟合。

Result: 半经验拟合显示余辉可由单一超相对论激波解释，但与标准余辉模型慢冷却机制预测不一致。早期同步自吸收频率ν_a应高于观测采样频率。引入τ_enh=500后，结构化喷流前向激波模型可合理描述射电颜色演化及多波段发射。

Conclusion: 光学深度增强可能来源于激波下游未经历Fermi I加速的冷电子群，与已有理论预期一致。本研究强调了系统性的多频率、多历元射电后随观测对极端天体事件的重要性。

Abstract: We present the observing campaign of the afterglow of GRB241025A, a gamma-ray burst (GRB) whose prompt emission has been simultaneously detected by Swift, Einstein Probe, Fermi/GBM, SVOM, Konus-Wind and VZLUSAT-2 3U CubeSat. Our multi-wavelength campaign comprises radio, near-infrared, Optical and X-ray observations. The afterglow was clearly detected in all bands. We performed a semi-empirical fit of the data, showing that the afterglow behaviour can be reasonably reproduced by a single component, i.e. an ultra-relativistic shock. However, the results from the semi-empirical fit are inconsistent with the predicted evolution from the standard afterglow model in the slow cooling regime. Specifically, we found that at early times the synchrotron self-absorption frequency $ν_a$ should be at higher frequencies with respect to the ones sampled by our campaign, in order to explain the observed colour evolution in radio, namely the spectral evolution in time. To reconcile the prediction from the standard model with the observed data set, we fit the observations with a semi-analytical model, including a multiplicative factor $τ_{enh}$ to the optical depth which, in turn, artificially increases $ν_a$. We found that the radio colour evolution, together with the near-infrared, optical and X-ray emission, can be described reasonably well by a forward shock from a structured jet, provided that the optical depth in the shocked material is enhanced by a factor $τ_{enh}=500$. We suggest that such enhancement in the optical depth can result from a population of cold electrons in the downstream material, i.e. electrons that were not accelerated by Fermi I process at the shock front, in agreement with the theoretical expectations previously reported in the literature. Overall, our work underscores the importance of systematic, multi-frequency, multi-epoch radio follow-ups of these extreme events.

</details>


### [132] [Testing Gravity with Binary Pulsars in the SKA Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02064)
*V. Venkatraman Krishnan,L. Shao,V. Balakrishnan,A. Carleo,M. Colom i Bernadich,A. Corongiu,A. Deller,P. C. C. Freire,M. Geyer,E. Hackmann,H. Hu,Z. Hu,M. Kramer,J. Kunz,K. Liu,M. E. Lower,X. Miao,D. Perrodin,D. S. Pillay,A. Possenti,S. Ransom,I. Stairs,B. Stappers,The SKA Pulsar Science Working Group*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文讨论了利用射电脉冲双星（包括SKA望远镜）进行强场引力测试的现状与前景，涵盖等效原理、引力辐射、黑洞假说等检验内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双星脉冲星是研究强场引力的天然实验室，SKA的高灵敏度将极大提升计时精度，发现更多相对论性脉冲星系统（包括脉冲星-黑洞双星），从而更深入地检验广义相对论及其他引力理论。

Method: 通过高精度脉冲星计时观测，分析已知和待发现的相对论性双星系统的轨道演化、自旋进动等特征，检验引力理论的各项预言。

Result: SKA将提升已知系统的测试精度，并通过巡天发现大量新的相对论性双星系统，可能包括脉冲星-黑洞双星，从而对宇宙审查假说和无毛定理等提供检验。

Conclusion: SKA在强场引力测试方面具有巨大潜力，需要具备特定的灵敏度和观测能力以实现这些科学目标。

Abstract: Binary (and trinary) radio pulsars are natural laboratories in space for understanding gravity in the strong field regime, with many unique and precise tests carried out so far, including the most precise tests of the strong equivalence principle and of the radiative properties of gravity. The Square Kilometre Array (SKA) telescope, with its high sensitivity in the Southern Hemisphere, will vastly improve the timing precision of recycled pulsars, allowing for a deeper search of potential deviations from general relativity (GR) in currently known systems. A Galactic census of pulsars will, in addition, will yield the discovery of dozens of relativistic pulsar systems, including potentially pulsar -- black hole binaries, which can be used to test the cosmic censorship hypothesis and the ``no-hair'' theorem. Aspects of gravitation to be explored include tests of strong equivalence principles, gravitational dipole radiation, extra field components of gravitation, gravitomagnetism, and spacetime symmetries. In this chapter, we describe the kinds of gravity tests possible with binary pulsar and outline the features and abilities that SKA must possess to best contribute to this science.

</details>


### [133] [Timing and spectral analysis of the 2025 outburst of 4U 1630$-$47 with \textit{NICER}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02228)
*Haifan Zhu,Mariano Méndez,Xiao Chen,Wei Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用NICER观测分析了2025年黑洞X射线双星4U 1630-47爆发期间的准周期振荡（QPOs）和毫赫兹准规则调制（QRM），发现QPO频率随爆发上升阶段增加，与盘参数相关；QRM表现为较弱的类心跳变率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞X射线双星4U 1630-47在2025年爆发期间的低频QPO和毫赫兹QRM的谱-时变特性，以理解其物理起源。

Method: 利用NICER观测数据，采用小波分析进行状态分离，对QPO和QRM进行谱-时变分析，并使用相位分辨的Hilbert-Huang变换分析盘参数与流量之间的关系。

Result: QPO中心频率从~0.24 Hz升至~3.43 Hz；含QPO时段具有更高的内盘温度和更低的diskbb归一化；QRM频率~0.07 Hz，分数rms振幅~4.7%，弱于2023年心跳态；内盘温度与X射线流量正相关，diskbb归一化反相关，Γ变化微弱。

Conclusion: 短时标谱-时变主要通过盘参数体现；瞬态QPO与上升阶段盘相关的短时变一致；毫赫兹QRM可能是在爆发峰值附近出现的较弱的类心跳变率模式。

Abstract: We analyzed \textit{NICER} observations of the 2025 outburst of the black hole X-ray binary 4U~1630$-$47 to investigate the spectral--timing properties of its transient low-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) and millihertz-scale quasi-regular modulation (QRM). During the rising phase of the outburst, the QPO centroid frequency increased from $\sim 0.24$ Hz to $\sim 3.43$ Hz. Wavelet-based state separation shows that the with-QPO intervals are associated with a higher inner disk temperature and a lower \texttt{diskbb} normalization than the without-QPO intervals, while the photon index ($Γ$) shows weaker changes within the uncertainties. Near the outburst peak, the source displayed a weak QRM at $\sim 0.07$ Hz with a fractional rms amplitude of $\sim 4.7\%$, lower than that of the heartbeat state observed in 2023. Phase-resolved Hilbert--Huang analysis shows that the inner disk temperature is positively correlated with the X-ray flux, the \texttt{diskbb} normalization is anticorrelated, and $Γ$ varies only weakly. Overall, the short-timescale spectral--timing variability is expressed most clearly through the disk-related parameters. The transient QPOs are therefore consistent with short-timescale disk-related variability during the rising phase, whereas the millihertz-scale QRM may represent a weaker heartbeat-like variability mode appearing near the outburst peak.

</details>


### [134] [SN 2020bij and a Possible Slow-Rise High-Velocity Subclass of Type IIP Supernovae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02253)
*Sondos Mohsen-Tanev,Iair Arcavi,Shahar Bracha,K. Azalee Bostroem,Griffin Hosseinzadeh,Jesper Sollerman,Claudia P. Gutiérrez,Priscila J. Pessi,Joseph Anderson,Mariusz Gromadzki,Avishay Gal-Yam,Daichi Hiramatsu,Jamison Burke,Koichi Itagaki,Ting-Wan Chen,D. Andrew Howell,Curtis McCully,Megan Newsome,Estefania Padilla Gonzalez,Craig Pellegrino*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文发现了一类新的IIn型超新星（SN 2020bij等5个事件），其特征为光变曲线缓慢上升至平台期且具有高膨胀速度，这些超新星不需要或仅需微弱的星周物质（CSM）相互作用即可解释，从而构成了IIn型超新星的新子类，可能与红超巨星晚期相对受限的质量损失有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解II型超新星爆炸特性与其前身星性质之间的关系，可为大质量恒星晚期演化阶段提供重要约束。近期发现部分IIn型超新星需要与星周物质相互作用才能解释其快速上升到平台期的光变曲线，而本研究则关注具有缓慢上升光变曲线的特例。

Method: 对SN 2020bij进行观测与分析，并从文献中识别出其他4个具有相似缓慢上升光变曲线和高膨胀速度的IIn型超新星（ASASSN-14kg、SN 2018fif、SN 2021yja、SN 2023axu），采用解析模型和数值模拟方法对这5个事件进行建模。

Result: 所有5个事件均可通过微弱至无CSM相互作用来解释，它们构成了一类新的IIn型超新星子类，可能与相对受限的星周物质有关。

Conclusion: 这些缓慢上升的IIn型超新星代表了一个新的子类，早期密集的光度和光谱观测将进一步描绘其物理性质，理解这类稀有事件对约束红超巨星晚阶段质量损失的多样性至关重要。

Abstract: Mapping how the explosion properties of Type II supernovae (SNe II) relate to the properties of their progenitors can provide strong constraints for understanding the final evolutionary stages of massive stars. Type IIP SNe, linked to the explosions of single red super-giant (RSG) stars, have recently been found to require some form of interaction with circumstellar material (CSM) to reproduce the rapid rise to the plateau often seen in their light curves. In this work, we present observations and analysis of the Type IIP SN 2020bij, characterized by a slow rise to its plateau as well as high expansion velocities. We identify four other SNe IIP from the literature (ASASSN-14kg, SN 2018fif, SN 2021yja and SN 2023axu) with similarly slowly rising light curves and find that they also show high expansion velocities. Using both analytical and numerical models, all five events can be explained with weak to no CSM interaction. We therefore propose that these events constitute a new subclass of Type IIP SNe which could be associated with relatively confined CSM. Early and dense photometric coverage of future SNe IIP together with early spectroscopic observations will further map this subclass and its physical properties. Understanding such rare events could be key to constraining the diversity of late-stage mass-loss in RSGs.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Sampling for Region-Aggregated Spatial Scan Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01451)
*Foad Namjoo,Drew McClelland,Michael Matheny,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 针对地理空间异常检测中区域聚合数据与点基扫描统计之间的不匹配问题，提出将每个区域均匀采样20-50个点并均匀分配数值的简单方法，相比传统质心法显著提升了统计效能且保持计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间扫描统计工具（如SaTScan）在处理区域聚合数据时，普遍将区域简化为质心点，这忽略了区域的空间范围，导致统计效能显著下降。作者旨在解决这一关键不匹配问题。

Method: 将每个空间区域用20-50个均匀采样的点代替，并将该区域的数值平均分配到这些采样点上，从而将区域聚合数据转换为点数据，再应用基于点的空间扫描统计。

Result: 该方法相比传统质心法显著提升了统计效能。收敛性分析表明，每个区域仅需少量采样点即可达到良好性能，保证了计算可扩展性。

Conclusion: 推荐将这种基于采样的转换方法作为对区域聚合数据应用点基空间扫描异常检测的默认处理方式。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in geospatial data is a crucial tool in geographic information science (GIS), with applications ranging from national security to public-health surveillance to the study of societal disparities. This work focuses on spatial scan statistics and addresses a key mismatch: spatial counts are typically aggregated into predefined regions (census tracts, zip codes, counties), whereas the most efficient scan algorithms operate on spatial point data. The standard remedy -- collapsing each region to its centroid, as in widely used tools such as SaTScan -- is convenient but, as we show, discards the region's spatial extent and causes a significant loss in statistical power. To resolve this, we propose a simple yet scalable fix: replace each spatial region with 20-50 points sampled uniformly from its geometry and spread the region's values evenly across them. This approach improves statistical power while maintaining computational tractability. A convergence analysis explains why so few samples per region suffice. We recommend this sampling-based conversion as the default way to apply point-based spatial scan statistics to region-aggregated data for anomaly detection.

</details>


### [136] [Sensitivity Analysis and Optimization of Stochastic Epidemic Models under Parameter Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01476)
*Nicholas R. Wu,Michael C. Fu*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文为离散时间随机流行病模型推导了无偏梯度估计量，以处理参数不确定性（如贝叶斯校准产生的分布），并分析了有限种群和时间范围对敏感性的影响，发现参数不确定性会降低敏感度并导致更保守的优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病模型的敏感性分析和优化通常假设参数已知，但实际中参数存在不确定性（如贝叶斯校准产生的分布）。作者旨在解决在参数不确定下如何有效进行敏感性分析和优化的问题。

Method: 推导无偏梯度估计量，估计有限时间范围内总感染人数对免疫比例(v)和接触率(β)的敏感性；与基于大种群的确定性极限近似进行对比；通过仿真实验分析参数不确定性对敏感性和优化策略的影响。

Result: 所提估计量对β的导数方差低于有限差分估计量，但对v的导数方差更高；参数不确定性降低了目标参数的敏感性，尤其是间接效应（如群体免疫）在参数不确定时减弱；引入参数不确定性导致更保守的干预策略。

Conclusion: 在随机流行病模型中考虑参数不确定性对敏感性分析和优化至关重要，忽视不确定性可能导致过度自信的干预政策，而纳入不确定性则产生更稳健的保守策略。

Abstract: To address sensitivity analysis and optimization for a discrete-time stochastic epidemic model, we derive unbiased gradient estimators that accommodate uncertainties represented as distributions over the parameters of interest, such as those arising from Bayesian calibration.
  Specifically, we estimate the sensitivity of total infections over a finite time horizon with respect to the proportion immunized ($v$) and the contact rate ($β$). Comparing the proposed estimators with deterministic limit approximations based on large populations reveals differences due to the finite population and time horizon. The estimators exhibit lower variance than finite-difference estimators for the derivative with respect to $β$, but higher variance for the derivative with respect to $v$. Simulation experiments indicate parameter uncertainty reduces sensitivity to the parameters of interest. In particular, indirect effects of vaccination, such as herd immunity, are less pronounced compared to when parameters are known. For optimization problems balancing intervention and infection costs, incorporating parametric uncertainty leads to more conservative policies.

</details>


### [137] [An unsupervised kernel norm monitoring for fault detection in a time series photovoltaic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01541)
*Victoria Jorry,Zina-Sabrina Duma,Satu-Pia Reinikainen,Heikki Haario,Lassi Roininen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核的无监督窗口式故障检测方法KNM（Kernel-based Norm Monitoring），用于光伏并网系统等连续工业过程的异常检测。该方法利用柯西核在无需先验故障知识的情况下，通过监测滑动窗口内数据点的范数变化来识别故障。在GPVS-Faults数据集上，KNM在传感器故障和局部遮荫两个场景中分别达到99.1%和98.3%的准确率，显著优于OCSVM、iForest和LOF等基线方法，并支持可解释的变量贡献分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际光伏系统中标注故障数据稀缺，监督学习方法受限；传统无监督基线方法（OCSVM、iForest、LOF）虽能处理非线性行为，但核方法超参数调优需依赖先验故障知识，缺乏通用性和实用性。

Method: 提出KNM（Kernel-based Norm Monitoring），一种基于核的无监督滑动窗口故障检测方法。通过核函数将数据映射到高维空间，计算窗口内数据点的范数作为异常评分，无需故障标签和先验知识。采用柯西核（Cauchy kernel）构建决策边界，并引入变量贡献图支持故障识别。

Result: 在GPVS-Faults数据集（IPPT模式）上，KNM在传感器故障场景达到99.1%准确率，局部遮荫场景达到98.3%准确率；最优基线方法（OCSVM）仅为93.5%。

Conclusion: KNM是一种通用、可解释且高效的工业过程无监督故障检测框架，在缺少标注数据的场景下显著优于传统方法，具有良好的泛化能力和实用价值。

Abstract: Grid-connected photovoltaic systems (GCPVS) are generally robust but remain susceptible to faults that can compromise energy conversion efficiency or raise safety concerns. Promptly and automatically detecting such anomalies is therefore essential for maintaining system reliability and performance. However, in practice, labeled fault data are rarely available in real-world deployments, which limits the applicability of supervised approaches. Conventional unsupervised baseline models, including a one-class support vector machine (OCSVM), isolation forest (iForest), and local outlier factor (LOF), are trained on normal operation data and assign anomaly scores reflecting how closely new observations resemble that baseline. Although these methods already accommodate non-linear behavior to varying degrees, kernel-based formulations offer further flexibility in shaping the decision boundary; however, tuning the kernel hyperparameters ordinarily requires some prior knowledge of the fault regime. We overcome this limitation by proposing kernel-based norm monitoring (KNM), a non-linear, unsupervised, window-based fault-detection method designed for continuous processes. Although the paper focuses on the GCPVS as a case study, KNM is a general-purpose monitoring framework applicable to a wide range of industrial processes. Using the Grid-connected PV System Faults (GPVS-Faults) dataset operating in intermediate power point tracking (IPPT) mode, KNM is evaluated in two fault scenarios, sensor faults and partial shading, against three benchmark techniques: OCSVM, iForest, and LOF. KNM achieves up to 99.1% and 98.3% accuracy on the two fault scenarios, respectively, using the Cauchy kernel, compared to 93.5% for the best-performing benchmark. The method is interpretable, and variable contribution plots are proposed to support fault identification.

</details>


### [138] [An Adaptive Glicko-2 Rating Framework for Probabilistic Football Forecasting and Season Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01722)
*Bich Van Nguyen,Nam Anh Tran*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应Glicko-2的足球比赛结果预测与赛季模拟框架，通过引入胜分差调整、统治力加权、结构性冲击、主场优势建模和有序Logit平局模型等足球特定机制，显著提升了预测准确性和赛季模拟的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo等评分系统虽简洁可解释，但未显式建模不确定性且忽略足球特定上下文信息（如平局的核心作用、球队实力动态变化等），亟需一种能融合足球领域知识、兼顾概率预测与赛季模拟的适应性评分框架。

Method: 以Glicko-2为基础，扩展加入五大足球特定机制：1）胜分差调整；2）统治力加权；3）结构性冲击；4）主场优势建模；5）有序Logit平局模型。将评分差异转化为胜-平-负概率，并通过蒙特卡洛采样模拟联赛剩余赛程。

Result: 该框架能够生成概率性足球预测，并实现联赛级别的赛季模拟，相比传统基线方法在预测准确性和模拟可靠性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 融合足球领域知识的自适应Glicko-2框架有效解决了足球比赛预测中实力动态变化、高度随机性和平局结构等问题，为概率预测和赛季模拟提供了实用且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Football match outcome prediction is a challenging problem because team strength changes over time, match outcomes contain a high level of randomness, and draws play a central role in the result structure. Classical rating systems such as Elo provide simple and interpretable dynamic summaries of team ability, but they do not explicitly model uncertainty and often ignore football-specific contextual information. This paper proposes an adaptive Glicko-2-based rating framework for probabilistic football forecasting and leaguelevel season simulation. The proposed framework extends the standard Glicko-2 model by incorporating football-specific mechanisms, including margin-of-victory adjustment, dominance weighting, structural shocks, home advantage modelling, and an ordered-logit draw model. The framework estimates latent team strength dynamically, converts rating differences into win-draw-loss probabilities, and uses these probabilities to simulate the remaining part of a league season through Monte Carlo sampling.

</details>


### [139] [Inverse Suitability: Identifying Condition Difficulty and Rider Skill from Behavioural Outcomes via Continuous-Item Response Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01961)
*Fabio Carucci*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出逆适切性（Inverse Suitability）模型，将项目反应理论（IRT）应用于户外运动条件评估，从行为结果中分离出参与者技能与环境内在难度，并通过合成数据验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的户外运动适切性评分（如风筝冲浪、滑翔伞、滑雪登山）依赖专家定义的曲线将环境条件映射为可行/不可行判定，但这些曲线混淆了条件的固有难度和面对该条件的人的技能水平。本文旨在从行为结果中分离这两种因素。

Method: 提出Inverse Suitability模型，基于连续项目IRT框架，对每个结果（参与者r、条件度量x、地点s、二元结果y）建模为P(y=1)=σ(a(θ_r-δ(x,s)))，其中θ_r为潜在技能，δ(x,s)为以物理专家曲线为先验的潜在难度函数，a为区分度参数。采用边际最大似然估计结合Gauss-Hermite求积法进行参数估计。

Result: 在合成数据上（80名参与者×30次结果），模型恢复潜在技能的相关性r=0.96，难度最小值定位误差在3个单位以内，留出法Brier技能得分比专家曲线基线提升+0.33。

Conclusion: 恢复的难度函数定义了可测量的场地级内在难度图谱，这是现有气象观测网络无法捕捉的。所有结果可通过单个命令在合成数据上复现，无需专有数据。

Abstract: Suitability scoring for outdoor activities (kitesurfing, paragliding, ski touring) maps environmental conditions to a go/no-go verdict via expert-defined curves. These curves conflate two distinct quantities: the intrinsic difficulty of a condition and the skill of the person facing it. We introduce Inverse Suitability, a continuous-item Item Response Theory (IRT) model that identifies both from behavioural outcomes alone. Each outcome is a triple (rider r, condition metric x at site s, binary outcome y); we model P(y=1) = sigma(a (theta_r - delta(x, s))), where theta_r is latent rider skill, delta(x, s) is a latent difficulty function anchored to a physics-derived expert curve as its prior, and a is a discrimination parameter. The formulation is strictly more general than a single suitability curve, which it recovers exactly when skill is integrated out under the population distribution. Parameters are estimated by marginal maximum likelihood with Gauss-Hermite quadrature; identification holds when the rider-by-condition incidence graph is connected, with a documented single-curve fallback otherwise. We validate via synthetic recovery: on a reference cohort (80 riders times 30 outcomes) the model recovers latent skill at r = 0.96, locates the difficulty minimum within 3 units of ground truth, and improves held-out Brier Skill Score by +0.33 over the expert-curve baseline. The recovered difficulty function defines a measurable, site-level construct, an intrinsic difficulty atlas, that existing meteorological observation networks do not capture. All results reproduce from a single command on synthetic data, requiring no proprietary observations.

</details>


### [140] [Convergence fragility in probit Bayesian kernel machine regression implemented in the bkmr R package for binary-outcome environmental mixture analyses: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02000)
*Akifumi Eguchi,Takayuki Kawashima,Tomotaka Momozaki,Tomoyuki Nakagawa*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文评估了bkmr包中probit BKMR的工作流程，发现拟合完成率高达99.8%（430/431），但满足R-hat≤1.01且ESS≥400的收敛标准仅占6.97%（30/430），表明拟合完成不等同于MCMC收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BKMR广泛用于二元结局的暴露混合物分析，但现有工作流中拟合完成并不保证MCMC收敛，需要通过仿真研究区分执行成功与收敛诊断。

Method: 使用bkmr::SimData()生成数据（二项分布，hfun=2, beta.true=0.5），bkmr::kmbayes()拟合模型，通过posterior包进行收敛诊断。4条链使用固定种子20260621生成链特异随机初始值，评估431个预设任务的拟合完成率和收敛指标。

Result: 431个任务中430个返回拟合对象（完成率99.8%），1个数值未完成。满足R-hat≤1.01的有55/431（12.8%），bulk-ESS≥400有85/431（19.7%），tail-ESS≥400有44/431（10.2%）。同时满足R-hat≤1.01且两种ESS≥400的仅30/431（6.97%），占430个完成拟合的6.98%。

Conclusion: probit BKMR的拟合完成不应等同于MCMC收敛。应用分析应报告链数、热身前/后迭代数、R-hat、bulk-ESS和tail-ESS，而非仅依赖固定迭代数或拟合完成状态。

Abstract: Background. Bayesian kernel machine regression (BKMR) is widely used for exposure-mixture analyses with binary outcomes through a probit extension. Because a bkmr fit can complete without providing adequate effective posterior information, simulation studies should separate execution success from MCMC convergence diagnostics. Methods. We evaluated the public bkmr probit workflow using bkmr::SimData() for data generation, bkmr::kmbayes() for model fitting, and posterior for convergence diagnostics. The balanced generator used family = "binomial", hfun = 2, beta.true = 0.5, ind = 1:2, and M = 4. SimData() generated the covariate as X = 3*cos(z1) + 2*rnorm(n). Four chains were initialized with chain-specific randomized starting values generated reproducibly from the fixed initial-value base seed 20260621. These values affected only the initial state of the sampler and did not alter the BKMR model, default priors, or Metropolis-Hastings proposals. Results. Of 431 prespecified tasks, 430 returned fitted objects and one task had a numerical non-completion. Diagnostic adequacy was limited: rank-normalized R-hat <= 1.01 threshold was achieved in 55/431 tasks, bulk-ESS >= 400 in 85/431, tail-ESS >= 400 in 44/431, and both ESS criteria in 44/431. The primary diagnostic criterion, R-hat at or below the 1.01 threshold with both bulk-ESS and tail-ESS >= 400, was met in 30/431 prespecified tasks, corresponding to 30/430 completed fits. Conclusions. Completion of probit BKMR fits in bkmr should not be equated with convergence of the retained MCMC draws. Applied analyses should report the number of chains, warmup and retained iterations, rank-normalized R-hat, bulk-ESS, and tail-ESS rather than rely on a fixed iteration count or on fit completion alone.

</details>


### [141] [Quaternion Nondecimated Wavelet Descriptors for Multiclass Breast Histology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02133)
*Sara Antonijevic,Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于四元数非抽取小波变换（QNDWT2D）的可解释框架，用于乳腺癌组织学分类。将RGB图像编码为纯四元数场，提取多尺度、方向性、颜色耦合的系数特征，在BACH数据集上使用SVM实现了良好的四类分类，无需预训练网络或外部数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H&E染色组织学图像中的诊断信息本质上是彩色的，传统的灰度化或RGB独立通道处理方法无法充分捕获颜色耦合信息。需要一种能够保持颜色作为单一向量量的可解释框架。

Method: 将RGB图像编码为纯四元数场，采用二维四元数非抽取小波变换（QNDWT2D）生成多尺度、方向性、颜色耦合的系数场。从中构建多组可解释特征族（染色平衡、小波能量、幅度异质性、四元数相位集中度、颜色轴几何、方向各向异性、方向熵、尺度依赖能量衰减），使用径向核SVM进行嵌套交叉验证分类。

Result: 在BACH四类数据集上实现平衡的类别识别，错误主要集中在相邻类别之间（正常与浸润很少混淆）。置换重要性分析表明方向性、相位集中度、各向异性、尺度和幅度变异性特征组均有贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架无需预训练网络、学习滤波器或外部数据库，提供了一个可重复、可解释的计算病理学基线方法，证明四元数与多尺度几何信息对分类有效。

Abstract: Breast histology images carry diagnostic information in color, texture, orientation, and tissue architecture across a range of scales. In H&E microscopy this information is inherently chromatic and is not fully recovered when the red, green, and blue (RGB) channels are reduced to grayscale or transformed as independent scalar images. We propose an interpretable quaternion nondecimated wavelet framework for breast histology classification. Each RGB image is encoded as a pure quaternion field, and a quaternion nondecimated wavelet transform in two dimensions (QNDWT2D) produces multiscale, directional, color-coupled coefficient fields on the original image grid, keeping color as a single vector quantity rather than three separate channels. From these coefficients we build interpretable feature families summarizing stain balance, wavelet energy, amplitude heterogeneity, quaternion phase concentration, color-axis geometry, directional anisotropy, orientation entropy, and scale-dependent energy decay, each tied to a histopathological property such as nuclear density or glandular organization. We evaluate the descriptors on the BreAst Cancer Histology (BACH) challenge, a balanced four-class set of normal, benign, in situ, and invasive tissue, using a radial-kernel support vector machine (SVM) with repeated nested cross-validation. The descriptors yield balanced recognition across classes, with errors concentrated among adjacent categories while normal and invasive are rarely reversed. Permutation importance shows that directional, phase-concentration, anisotropy, scale, and amplitude-variability groups all contribute, indicating that the classifier draws on genuine quaternion and multiscale geometry rather than global color alone. The framework uses no pretrained networks, learned filters, or external databases, offering a reproducible, interpretable baseline for computational pathology.

</details>


### [142] [Value-of-Information Analysis for External Validation of Risk Prediction Models in Multicenter Studies and Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02321)
*Laure Wynants,Kim Zhipei Wang,Sabine Grimm,Andrea Gabrio,Andrew Vickers,Ewout Steyerberg,Ben Van Calster,Mohsen Sadatsafavi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文扩展了预期信息价值（EVPI）方法，考虑了多中心研究和荟萃分析中的中心间异质性，并引入EVPIglobal、EVPIcluster_j、EVPIcluster和EVPPIcluster,prevalence等指标，通过ADNEX模型在36个中心的数据进行验证，量化了不确定性对净获益的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部验证研究样本量有限，导致预测模型的净获益（NB）是否优于默认策略存在不确定性。现有EVPI方法仅关注单一研究，忽略了中心间异质性，无法准确量化多中心场景下的不确定性后果。

Method: 将EVPI和EVPPI扩展至多中心研究和荟萃分析，区分全局与局部最优策略、观测与未观测中心，定义EVPIglobal、EVPIcluster_j、EVPIcluster和EVPPIcluster,prevalence四个指标，并在MetaNB R包中实现。通过ADNEX模型在36个中心的系统综述数据进行实证分析。

Result: ADNEX模型全局最优（EVPIglobal=0）；荟萃分析借用了观测中心信息，EVPIcluster_j通常低于仅用单中心数据；未观测中心中有0.03概率默认策略更优；消除所有中心的不确定性每年可避免1134个假阳性，仅消除患病率不确定性每年可避免158个假阳性。

Conclusion: EVPI扩展指标能够分离不同来源的不确定性，量化进一步验证的必要性。考虑临床效用的不确定性和中心间异质性对于决定是否进行额外验证研究至关重要。

Abstract: External validation studies have finite sample sizes, creating uncertainty about whether a prediction model's Net Benefit (NB) exceeds default strategies' NB. The expected value of perfect information (EVPI) quantifies consequences of uncertainty. Current EVPI methods focus on single studies, ignoring between-center heterogeneity. We extend EVPI and expected value of partial perfect information (EVPPI) to account for between-cluster heterogeneity in multicenter studies and meta-analyses. We distinguish between the global and local optimal strategy and between observed and unobserved clusters. We define EVPIglobal, EVPIcluster_j, EVPIcluster, and EVPPIcluster,prevalence, implemented in the MetaNB R package, and illustrate them using a systematic review across 36 centers of the ADNEX model for ovarian cancer diagnosis. Assuming one global decision regarding ADNEX adoption, there is no need for further data to confirm ADNEX is superior overall (EVPIglobal 0). Meta-analysis borrows information across observed clusters, resulting in consistent local superiority of ADNEX and nonzero but typically lower EVPIcluster_j than when considering local data alone. There is 0.03 probability default strategies are superior in unobserved centers. Eliminating uncertainty on performance and prevalence in each (EVPIcluster) would gain 1134 net avoided false positives (FP) per year, assuming 350000 tumors annually with 20% malignancies. Determining only local prevalence with certainty (EVPPIcluster, prevalence) would gain net 158 avoided FP per year. EVPI extensions disentangle sources of uncertainty and quantify the need for further validation to determine the global or locally optimal strategy. Considering uncertainty and heterogeneity in clinical utility across clusters is essential to decide whether additional validation studies are warranted.

</details>


### [143] [Instrumented difference-in-differences under case-control sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02327)
*Tran Trong Khoi Le,Emilie Sbidian,Tat-Thang Vo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于病例-对照设计的工具变量差分法(iDiD)，该方法允许工具变量对结局存在时间恒定的直接效应，并在回顾性抽样校正后可作为趋势尺度上的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工具变量方法依赖于排他性约束假设，即工具变量不能通过暴露以外的路径直接影响结局。在病例-对照设计中，这一假设常被违背，限制了IV方法的应用。本文旨在放宽这一假设，使存在时间恒定直接效应的候选IV仍能被有效使用。

Method: 基于结构均值建模，提出仪器化差分法(iDiD)，针对回顾性病例-对照数据，通过校正回顾性抽样引起的选择偏倚，使存在时间恒定直接效应的候选变量在趋势尺度上成为有效工具变量。

Result: 通过大量模拟实验验证了有限样本性能，并应用于法国国家医保数据库，评估银屑病生物治疗引发严重感染的风险。

Conclusion: iDiD方法在保留病例-对照设计优势的同时，放宽了传统IV的排他性约束，为存在直接效应的工具变量提供了一种可行的推断框架。

Abstract: Case-control designs are fundamental in epidemiology for the efficient study of rare outcomes. Although instrumental variable (IV) methods have been extended to this setting to address unmeasured confounding, they typically rely on the exclusion restriction assumption, which may be violated when the IV candidates directly affect the outcome through pathways independent of the exposure. In this paper, we propose a novel instrumented difference-in-differences (iDiD) approach tailored to case-control designs. Grounded in structural mean modeling, the proposed method accommodates IV candidates that have time-invariant direct effect on the outcome. When retrospective case-control datasets are collected, the candidate can still be used as a valid instrument on the trend scale when selection bias induced by retrospective sampling is efficiently taken into account. We assess finite-sample performance of this method through extensive simulations, then apply it to evaluate the risk of serious infection of biologic treatments for psoriasis, using French national claim database.

</details>


### [144] [Masked complex non-decimated wavelet features for patient-level classification of contrast-enhanced mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02394)
*Sara Antonijevic,Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于掩膜复数非抽取小波特征库与弹性网络逻辑回归的CESM分类框架，在无数据泄露的评估体系下，发现低能量图像与对比增强图像在患者级别的恶性检测AUC上无统计学差异，但二者通过不同可解释通道编码恶性信息，为未来CESM分类器提供了透明且无泄漏的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CESM分类器构建中存在两个未解决的核心问题：(1) 两种图像（低能量图像与对比增强图像）是否携带相当的恶性信号；(2) 如何将单个患者的多个图像合并为单一决策。此外，现有方法普遍存在数据泄露问题（图像级交叉验证导致同一患者图像分入训练集和测试集），导致性能被高估。

Method: 采用掩膜复数非抽取小波特征库（masked complex non-decimated wavelet feature bank）结合弹性网络逻辑回归分类器，在患者分组嵌套交叉验证（patient-grouped nested cross-validation）下评估，并使用患者聚类bootstrap推断，在CDD-CESM数据集（1,880张图像，308名患者）上进行无泄漏评估。

Result: 在正常vs恶性检测任务中，两种图像类型在患者级别AUC上无统计差异；对比图像患者级别AUC为0.874（95% CI 0.827-0.918），低能量图像与之无显著差异；二者通过不同可解释通道编码恶性信息（低能量图像：相位相干性；对比图像：幅度分布）；所提框架在患者级别与预训练ResNet-50表现匹配。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个透明、无数据泄露的基线，可用于未来CESM分类器的性能比较；小波表示的每个预测器都具有显式物理意义，而深度表示则不可直接解释。

Abstract: Contrast-enhanced spectral mammography (CESM) acquires two images of each breast, a low-energy image and a recombined contrast image, but two questions central to building a classifier on them remain unsettled: whether the two image types carry comparable malignancy signal, and how a patient's several images should be combined into a single decision. Both are hard to answer reliably, because most published CESM classifiers split cross-validation folds at the image level, letting images of the same patient fall in both training and test sets and inflating reported performance. We pair a masked complex non-decimated wavelet feature bank with an elastic-net logistic classifier, evaluated under repeated patient-grouped nested cross-validation with patient-cluster bootstrap inference on the CDD-CESM dataset (1,880 images, 308 patients); under this leakage-free evaluation the inflation from testing on previously seen patients is negligible. On normal-versus-malignant detection, the two acquisitions are statistically indistinguishable in patient-level AUC under the proposed evaluation framework. Under single-image fusion the contrast image reaches a patient-level AUC of 0.874 (95% CI 0.827-0.918) and the low-energy image is statistically indistinguishable from it, yet the two encode malignancy through disjoint, interpretable channels: phase coherence on the low-energy image and magnitude distribution on the contrast image. The framework matches a pretrained ResNet-50 representation at the patient level, but whereas the frozen deep representation is not directly interpretable at the level of individual predictors, every predictor in the wavelet representation carries an explicit physical meaning. The result is a transparent, leakage-free baseline against which future CESM classifiers can be measured.

</details>
